1. 🔴「**The traditional approach remains preferable for most North American orthodontists because of its stability and predictability.**」
2. 術前 decompensation 可讓術中達到理想 intercuspation：〔Ch21.11〕「the teeth should be **decompensated, leveled, and aligned to allow ideal intercuspation during surgery**」。
3. 複雜三維矯正、缺牙、transverse discrepancy 者，**術後咬合更穩定可預測**〔Ch06.2〕。

**■ Surgery-first 的缺點（課本明列）**
1. **術後矯正期反而拉長 8 個月**（總時間才是縮短的）。
2. 適應症窄（見禁忌症清單）。
3. 時效壓力：**術後矯正必須 2 週內啟動**，否則失去 transient demineralization 的窗口。

> **⚠ 關於 transitional occlusion**：使用者的認知（surgery-first 術後會先進入一個「過渡性咬合」，再由術後矯正收尾）在概念上與課本描述相容 —— 課本說術後矯正是用來「treat the **residual malocclusion**」〔Ch06.2〕 —— 但**「transitional occlusion」這個術語本身在指定的五個章節中完全沒有出現**。口試若被問到，建議答：「課本以 *residual malocclusion* 描述術後待矯正的咬合；transitional occlusion 是臨床／長庚文獻的慣用語，Neligan 與 Grabb 此二章未給定義。」

---

### §2.7 骨骼成熟度怎麼判定 🟡
我的認知：hand-wrist radiograph、cervical vertebral maturation (CVM)、連續 cephalogram 比對。

課本：

> **⚠ 判定「方法」查無：課本查無明確出處**（已查：Grabb 9e Ch29 Orthognathic Surgery、Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11、Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch06.2、Grabb 9e Ch28、Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch26）。
> 五個章節全文檢索 **"hand-wrist"／"carpal"／"cervical vertebral maturation"／"serial cephalogram" 皆零命中**，因此**課本並未主推任何一種影像判定法**。課本一律用「**年齡**」與「**臨床／矯正科判斷**」來界定骨骼成熟。**不要編造課本主推 CVM 或 hand-wrist film。**

**■ 課本實際給的是「年齡」（🟡 數字清單）**

〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11〕🔴 —— **最明確、最該背的一句**
- 「Orthognathic surgery is performed best once **facial skeletal maturity** has been achieved so that the skeletal movements performed during surgery will have **long-term stability** and not change as the facial skeleton matures. **This is generally at a minimum of 14–16 years for females and 17–21 years for males.**」

〔Grabb 9e Ch28〕
- 「**Facial skeletal maturity is completed as early as 15 years of age in adolescent girls, though usually later in adolescent boys.**」

> **兩本不一致 → 兩個都寫**：女性 **14–16 歲（Neligan）／最早 15 歲（Grabb Ch28）**；男性 **17–21 歲（Neligan）／較女性晚（Grabb Ch28）**。

**■ 課本給的「成熟順序」（可用來取代影像判定法回答）**
〔Neligan Ch21.11〕🔴「Although there is significant variability between genders and among individuals, **skeletal maturation generally progresses in a cranial-to-caudal direction** with the **cranial vault** reaching close to its final size in **early adolescence**, then the **upper face** in the **early teen years**, the **maxilla** in the **mid teen years**, and the **mandible** in the **late teen years**.」
→ 這解釋了為何**下顎（BSSO）要等最久**，也是「男性等到 17–21 歲」的理由。

**■ 課本給的「臨床判準」**
〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕
- 🔴「Patients are `**ready for jaw surgery**` when they are **skeletally mature** and when their **teeth have been orthodontically aligned in the alveolar bone**.」
- **例外（可提前開刀者）**：「**Rare exceptions can be made for growing patients that need an early orthognathic surgery to correct a severe skeletal malocclusion or treat obstructive sleep apnea**, knowing that **another jaw surgery may be needed at the time of skeletal maturity**. This second orthognathic surgery after early jaw surgery is referred to as a `**finishing osteotomy**`.」（🔴 "finishing osteotomy" 這個名詞值得背。）
- **矯正科的角色**：「Orthodontists take advantage of this active phase of development through their use of braces, palatal expanders, and various external devices…**For this reason, surgical intervention is usually delayed until skeletal maturity is reached and orthodontic movements are no longer effective.**」〔Neligan Ch21.11〕

**■ 相關的「等到骨骼成熟」原則（其他章節，可佐證）**
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕**SARPE**：「**Once skeletal maturity has been reached**, however, the **suture must be surgically released** for the process to be successful.」（該章 Q3 解析：25 歲病人「well past skeletal maturity and fusion of the palatine suture」→ 必須 SARPE。）
- 〔Neligan Ch21.11〕**Condylar hyperplasia**：11 篇 289 例系統性回顧 —— 「**the optimal timing for intervention is late adolescence or early adulthood, right after skeletal maturity**, to allow the disease process to burn out and to prevent compensatory soft-tissue changes」；單做正顎 relapse 高，**合併 condylectomy（至少切除 3 mm condyle）** relapse 顯著較低。
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch28／Neligan Ch26〕**CFM**：Pruzansky type I 追蹤觀察，若有 malocclusion **延到 late teenage years 骨骼成熟後**才手術；alloplastic TMJ 重建、bimaxillary orthognathic 亦一律 **after facial skeletal maturity**。〔Neligan Ch26〕「If two-jaw surgery is to be performed for CFM, a **two-splint (intermediate splint) technique** is used and the technique is **usually deferred until skeletal maturity**.」
- 〔Neligan Ch26〕**早期 MDO 不能免除日後正顎**：「While ramus lengthening with distraction osteogenesis and orthodontics can improve the occlusal cant during mixed dentition, **it does not reduce the need for orthognathic surgery at skeletal maturity**.」

---
