# 三、OSA 的手術治療　〔林政輝〕🔴 手冊 0 筆

Neligan Vol 3 若有 OSA 章節即用之；否則 Grabb 的相關章。

> **【查證前提｜務必先看】**
> 我已把 **Grabb 9e 章節資料夾（115 章全部列出）** 與 **Neligan 5e Vol 3 資料夾（全部列出）** 逐一清點：**兩本書都沒有任何一章是專講 OSA / sleep apnea / snoring 的**。OSA 的內容只散在以下幾章，而且是「附帶提到」，不是系統性教學：
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch29 Orthognathic Surgery〕
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch28 Craniofacial Microsomia and Principles of Craniofacial Distraction〕
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch27 Syndromic Craniosynostosis〕
> - 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11 Cleft and craniofacial orthognathic surgery〕← **唯一有 MMA 成效數字的一章**
> - 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch26 Craniofacial microsomia〕
> - 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28 Robin sequence〕← 小兒 OSA 的主力章節
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch54 Functional Considerations in Esthetic Rhinoplasty〕（已查，**完全沒有 OSA 內容**，只談 nasal valve／nasal obstruction）
>
> 以下所有「查無」都是我實際 grep 過全章原文後的結論，**沒有用我自己的記憶補任何一個字**。
> 全書級關鍵字掃描結果（以上七章全文）：`Epworth` 0 次、`Friedman staging` 0 次、`Fujita` 0 次、`Mallampati` 0 次、`UPPP／uvulopalatopharyngoplasty` 0 次、`Stanford` 0 次、`adenotonsillectomy／tonsillectomy` 0 次、`genioglossus advancement` 0 次、`hyoid suspension` 0 次。
> （註：Grabb Ch54 參考文獻裡出現的 "Friedman O." 是作者姓名，**不是** Friedman staging。）

### §3.1 ⭐ 診斷與嚴重度 🟡
我的認知：PSG 是 gold standard；AHI 分級 mild 5–15、moderate 15–30、severe >30；Epworth Sleepiness Scale。
要確認：課本的分界與 apnea／hypopnea 的定義。

課本：

**（一）AHI 的 mild／moderate／severe 分界 → ⚠ 課本查無明確出處**
⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Grabb 9e Ch27／Ch28／Ch29／Ch54、Neligan Vol3 Ch21.11／Ch26／Ch28 全文；搜過 `AHI`、`apnea-hypopnea index`、`hypopnea`、`mild`、`moderate`、`severe`、`severity`、`polysomnograph`）。
→ **兩本書都沒有列出 AHI 5／15／30 的三級分界表。**「mild／moderate／severe OSA」這幾個詞在課本裡是**當形容詞直接用**，從未被定義成數字。

**（二）apnea 與 hypopnea 的定義 → ⚠ 課本查無明確出處**
⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查同上；搜過 `apnea`、`hypopnea`、`desaturation`、`oxygen saturation`）。
→ 課本從未定義「呼吸中止 ≥10 秒」「氣流下降 ≥30% 併 3–4% desaturation」這類 AASM 定義。

- 課本唯一提供的、屬於**新生兒**的客觀閾值（不可外推到成人）：
  「Initial assessment should include **continuous pulse oximetry** while the child is awake, sleeping, and feeding. The monitoring time required during sleep should be **a minimum of 12 hours for neonates**... **Desaturations are defined as having any single oxygen saturation value less than 80% at any time, or less than 90% for 5% or more of the monitored time.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28 Robin sequence〕

**（三）PSG 的地位 → ✅ 課本有，且明確支持你的認知**
- 「Postnatal diagnostics have increasingly emphasized the role of **polysomnography (PSG) for functional airway assessment** and **endoscopic studies for anatomic airway assessment**, as both provide **distinct information** to guide management.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
  → 課本的框架是 **PSG＝功能性評估、endoscopy＝解剖性評估，兩者互補**，這是比「PSG is gold standard」更能得分的講法。
- 「PSG has been used **not only in preoperative evaluation but also as a tool to evaluate the success of various interventions**.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- **Split-night sleep study**：「A valuable evaluation tool is a **split-night sleep study**, during which the patient is observed **without a nasopharyngeal airway for the first half** of the study and then **with a nasopharyngeal airway in place for the remaining time**... If OSA resolves with a nasopharyngeal airway in place, this suggests that the **obstruction is isolated to the tongue base**.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- 「Patients with signs of OSA who are stable should also undergo a polysomnogram (PSG). **A PSG provides information regarding the severity and type (central vs obstructive) of apneic episodes.** While obstructive apneas can be related to tongue-based airway obstruction or pharyngeal collapse, **central apneas may indicate brain stem compression or untreated intracranial hypertension.** PSG can also identify **"spurious" central apneas**, which occasionally occur in the obstructive setting, and are relieved by treatments that target the obstruction.」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
- 「In patients with symptoms of obstructive sleep apnea, **sleep studies (polysomnography) can define the degree of the respiratory dysfunction.** Polysomnography, along with the interpretation of clinical symptoms and endoscopic findings, can be invaluable in **determining whether surgical intervention (i.e., mandibular distraction) is indicated**.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch26〕
- 成人 orthognathic 端：「Finally, a history of snoring or obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) is important to note. **If there is any question regarding OSA, a formal sleep study and evaluation is obtained prior to developing a treatment plan.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11〕
- 「Any history of tachypnea, apnea, or snoring **warrants further evaluation with polysomnography** and consideration of otolaryngology referral for upper airway endoscopy.」〔Grabb 9e Ch28〕

**（四）Epworth Sleepiness Scale → ⚠ 課本查無明確出處**
⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：上述七章全文；搜過 `Epworth`、`sleepiness`、`questionnaire`）。**兩本書 0 次命中。**
→ 口試若要提，只能當作「臨床上的 subjective screening tool」帶過，**不能說是課本內容**。

### §3.2 ⭐ 手術階梯（Stanford protocol）🟡
我的認知：
- **Phase I**：UPPP、genioglossus advancement、hyoid suspension、（兒童）tonsillectomy
- **Phase II**：MMA（maxillomandibular advancement）
要確認：課本怎麼分期？有沒有列 Phase I 的成功率？

課本：

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Grabb 9e Ch27／Ch28／Ch29／Ch54、Neligan Vol3 Ch21.11／Ch26／Ch28 全文；搜過 `Stanford`、`phase I`、`phase II`、`UPPP`、`uvulopalatopharyngoplasty`、`genioglossus advancement`、`hyoid`、`suspension`、`tonsillectomy`、`adenotonsillectomy`、`protocol`）。**

- **Stanford protocol 這個名詞、Phase I／Phase II 的分期、以及 Phase I 的成功率，在 Grabb 9e 與 Neligan 5e Vol3 中完全不存在。**
- `UPPP` / `uvulopalatopharyngoplasty`：0 次命中。
- `genioglossus advancement`、`hyoid suspension`：0 次命中（`genioglossus` 只在 Neligan Ch28 出現 5 次，且**全部是 Robin sequence 做 tongue–lip adhesion 時的 genioglossus release／detachment**，與成人 Phase I 手術無關）。
- `tonsillectomy` / `adenotonsillectomy`：0 次命中。

**課本實際有的「階梯」是完全不同的兩套，考試回答請用這兩套（下方 §3.5 詳列）：**
1. **小兒 Robin sequence 的階梯**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕：prone positioning → nasopharyngeal (NP) airway → CPAP → **TLA（tongue–lip adhesion）或 MDO（mandibular distraction osteogenesis）** → tracheostomy。
2. **Syndromic craniosynostosis 的階梯**〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕：CPAP（mild–moderate）／tracheostomy（severe, 新生兒期）→ **midface advancement（Le Fort III / monobloc）**。

> 📌 **考場策略**：被問到 Stanford protocol，可以答「這是 sleep surgery 文獻的分期（Riley–Powell–Guilleminault），但 Grabb 與 Neligan 並未收錄；這兩本書處理 OSA 的主軸是 **skeletal advancement（MMA、MDO、midface advancement）**」——既不失分，又能立刻把戰場拉回課本有寫的 §3.3。

### §3.3 MMA 的成效 🔴
我的認知：surgical success（AHI 下降 >50% 且 <20）約 85–90%，cure rate 約 40–45%；前移幅度約 10 mm。**這組數字我很沒把握。**

課本：

🔴 **課本原文整段照抄**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11 Cleft and craniofacial orthognathic surgery，"Obstructive sleep apnea" 一節〕：

> **Obstructive sleep apnea**
> There are numerous anatomical causes for obstructive sleep apnea, but regardless of specific etiology, **maxillomandibular advancement with counterclockwise plane rotation can significantly improve obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) symptoms** (Fig. 21.11.24). **Maxillary advancement opens the velopharynx while tensioning the soft palate, and mandibular advancement anteriorly positions the tongue to increase the patency of the velopharynx. Both movements increase the cross-sectional area of the oropharyngeal airway.** Multiple systematic reviews of **462–627 patients** concluded that maxillomandibular advancement significantly improves OSA symptoms.^59–61^ **Apnea–hypopnea index (AHI) decreased by 80.1%, and respiratory disturbance index decreased by 64.6%.**^60^ **Based on AHI, over 85% of patients improved following surgery, and 38%–43% were completely cured, as defined by an AHI <5.**^59,60^ **Patients with a lower preoperative AHI were more likely to be cured of OSA, while those with higher preoperative AHI had greater percentage improvements.**^59^ **Younger age, lower BMI, and greater degree of maxillary advancement were also associated with greater surgical success.**^59^ **Patients with a maxillary advancement of 9.5 mm had significantly improved outcomes compared to patients advanced 7.9 mm.**^59^ Complications include facial paresthesia, speech abnormalities, swallowing difficulty, and systemic cardiac and pulmonary complications, but overall **complication rates remain low at 1%–3%**.^59^ **While relapse of the maxillary or mandibular advancement is reported to occur in 10%–20% of patients, this generally does not translate to regression of symptoms or worsened AHI.**^59^

**課本圖說（也是原文照抄，這是課本唯一給出的「前移幅度」實例）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11, Figure 21.11.24〕：

> **(A–C)** This is a preoperative photo of a patient who exemplifies the beneficial effects of ideal skeletal support on facial soft tissue. The patient had a **bimaxillary advancement of 10 mm and an advancement genioplasty of 8 mm to treat obstructive sleep apnea**. **(D–F)** Despite the significant advancement resulting in excessive SNA and SNB angles, facial form is improved postoperatively as the improvement in the soft tissue far outweighs the iatrogenic SNA and SNB abnormalities created by his sleep apnea treatment...

**課本引用的三篇 meta-analysis（可直接報出來，屬 EBM 加分）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11 References 59–61〕：

> 59. **Holty JE, Guilleminault C.** Maxillomandibular advancement for the treatment of obstructive sleep apnea: a systematic review and meta-analysis. *Sleep Med Rev.* 2010;14:287–297.
> 60. **Zaghi S, Holty JE, Certal V, et al.** Maxillomandibular advancement for treatment of obstructive sleep apnea: a meta-analysis. *JAMA Otolaryngol Head Neck Surg.* 2016;142:58–66.
> 61. **John CR, Gandhi S, Sakharia AR, James TT.** Maxillomandibular advancement is a successful treatment for obstructive sleep apnoea: a systematic review and meta-analysis. *Int J Oral Maxillofac Surg.* 2018;47:1561–1571.

---

#### ⚠️ 你的認知 vs 課本：**必須修正的三點**

| 項目 | 你原本的認知 | **課本〔Neligan Vol3 Ch21.11〕** | 判定 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **成功率** | surgical success（AHI↓>50% 且 <20）約 **85–90%** | **「over 85% of patients improved」**——課本用的字是 **improved**，並**沒有**引用「AHI 下降 >50% 且 <20」這個 Sher criteria 定義 | 🟡 **數字對，定義錯**。課本沒寫 surgical success 的定義 |
| **Cure rate** | 約 **40–45%** | **38%–43%**，且明確定義 **cure ＝ AHI <5** | 🟢 **幾乎正確，但請改口為 38–43%**，並記住 cure 的定義是 AHI <5 |
| **前移幅度** | 約 **10 mm** | 課本**沒有給「建議前移幾 mm」的通則**；只給兩個具體數據：① **maxillary advancement 9.5 mm 的結果顯著優於 7.9 mm**；② 案例圖為 **bimaxillary advancement 10 mm + advancement genioplasty 8 mm** | 🟡 **10 mm 可以講，但要說成「案例級／趨勢級」**，不要講成課本訂的標準值 |

#### ✅ 課本額外給的、你原本沒有的「加分子彈」
1. **機轉必背**：maxillary advancement → 打開 velopharynx 並 **tension the soft palate**；mandibular advancement → 把 **tongue 往前拉**；兩者共同 **增加 oropharyngeal airway 的 cross-sectional area**。
2. **AHI 平均下降 80.1%；RDI 平均下降 64.6%**（Zaghi 2016）。
3. **Counterclockwise (逆時針) occlusal plane rotation 是課本明講要合併做的**：「Counterclockwise rotation... Also useful in **maximizing airway opening when treating obstructive sleep apnea**. ... **If performing counterclockwise plane rotation, mandibular first surgery is recommended.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11, CASS 規劃表〕→ **這是很好的追問防線：做 MMA 治 OSA 要 CCW rotation，且建議 mandible-first。**
4. **預後因子**：preop AHI 低 → 較容易 cure；preop AHI 高 → 改善百分比較大；**younger age、lower BMI、greater maxillary advancement → surgical success 較好**。
5. **併發症率僅 1%–3%**；**relapse 10%–20%，但通常不會造成症狀復發或 AHI 惡化**。
6. **Class II 病人的適應症**〔Neligan Vol3 Ch21.11〕：「**Documented severe snoring or obstructive sleep apnea would warrant bimaxillary advancement to improve the upper airway.**」→ 即使 occlusion 單靠 mandibular surgery 就能矯正，有 OSA 就該做 **bimaxillary（雙顎）前移**。

### §3.4 阻塞部位的評估 🟡
我的認知：Friedman staging、Mallampati、Fujita classification、DISE（drug-induced sleep endoscopy）。
要確認：課本主推哪一套？

課本：

**（一）Friedman staging／Mallampati／Fujita classification → ⚠ 課本查無明確出處**
⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Grabb 9e Ch27／Ch28／Ch29／Ch54、Neligan Vol3 Ch21.11／Ch26／Ch28 全文；搜過 `Friedman`、`Mallampati`、`Fujita`、`staging`、`tonsil`、`palate grade`）。**三者皆 0 次命中。**（Grabb Ch54 reference 48 的 "Friedman O." 是作者姓名，非 staging。）

**（二）DISE（drug-induced sleep endoscopy）→ ✅ 課本有，而且是課本唯一提名的評估工具**
- 「Traditional **awake nasoendoscopy can underdiagnose the presence of moderate to severe airway obstruction** in patients with RS. In response to this limitation, **drug-induced sleep endoscopy has been developed to emulate the loss of tone and resulting airway obstruction that are observed during physiologic sleep.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- 課本的 **Algorithm 28.2「Surgical Evaluation Pathway」第一步就是**：「**Drug-induced sleep endoscopy and bronchoscopy**」→ 再分流為 severe secondary airway lesion（→ tracheostomy）／nonsyndromic（→ TLA or MDO）／syndromic（→ MDO or tracheostomy）。〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕

**（三）課本「主推」的阻塞部位評估法 → 是 endoscopy 的三分法，不是任何評分表**
- 「**Endoscopic evaluation in RS should include both nasoendoscopy and bronchoscopy** to demonstrate the proper level of obstruction. **There are three main subdivisions: no visible obstruction, tongue base obstruction, or infraglottic obstruction.** Care must be taken **not to stop after visualizing a tongue base obstruction**, since **synchronous airway lesions occur in 28% to over one-third of patients**.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- 「If the nasoendoscopy... demonstrates **no visible obstruction, one must suspect a central nervous system or pulmonary disorder**...」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- 「**Bronchoscopy is indicated to rule out a concomitant subglottic airway obstruction.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
- **Split-night PSG 也被課本當成定位工具**：OSA 在放置 NP airway 後消失 ⇒ 阻塞侷限於 **tongue base**。〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕（見 §3.1）
- 「In patients with respiratory insufficiency or sleep apnea, **endoscopy is indicated to document the site of obstruction**. In bilateral CFM... there can be significant **tongue-based airway obstruction** that can lead to life-threatening **retroglossal narrowing** secondary to mandibular deficiency. **Endoscopy can also visualize the remainder of the tracheobronchial tree to rule out other sites of obstruction.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch26〕
- 「In all patients, **a comprehensive airway evaluation should proceed**, including **the nasal passages** to rule out choanal atresia/stenosis, **the oral cavity/oropharynx** to rule out tongue-based airway obstruction, and **the remainder of the airway** to rule out tracheal pathologies (ie, stenosis, tracheomalacia, **tracheal sleeve**, reactive airway disease, secondary signs of gastroesophageal reflux).」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕另註：syndromic 病人的 **choanal stenosis 來自 midfacial hypoplasia 造成的環周狹窄與 high-arched palate**，不是先天 choanal atresia。

> 📌 **一句話答法**：「課本沒有主推任何一套評分表（Friedman／Mallampati／Fujita 皆未收錄）；Grabb 與 Neligan 主推的是 **PSG（功能）＋ endoscopy（解剖）雙軌**，而 endoscopy 要做到 **nasoendoscopy ＋ bronchoscopy**，因為 **synchronous airway lesion 高達 28%–1/3**；清醒 endoscopy 會低估，故發展出 **DISE**。」

### §3.5 治療的先後 🟢
我的認知：CPAP 是第一線，手術給 CPAP 失敗或不耐受者；兒童 OSA 第一線是 adenotonsillectomy。

課本：

🟢 **逐項判定**

**① 「CPAP 是第一線，手術給 CPAP 失敗或不耐受者」→ ⚠ 部分正確，但課本沒有這樣的成人通則**
- **成人**：⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查七章全文；搜過 `CPAP`、`continuous positive airway pressure`、`first-line`、`conservative`）。**Grabb Ch29 與 Neligan Ch21.11 的 CPAP 命中數皆為 0**——這兩章講成人 orthognathic／MMA 時，**完全沒有提到 CPAP，也沒有寫「手術保留給 CPAP 失敗者」**。
- **小兒／syndromic**：✅ **課本明確支持「positive airway pressure 先於手術」**——
  - 「**Mild to moderate OSA can often be managed by continuous positive airway pressure ventilation as definitive therapy, or a bridge to surgery.**」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
  - 「**Severe OSA early in life is often managed by tracheostomy; however, many patients are managed by positive airway pressure support.** Unfortunately, **OSA tends to worsen over time**...」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
  - 「After delivery, the airway must be secured, and **in patients with severe OSA, a tracheostomy is an excellent option. Mild to moderate OSA can be managed by positive pressure airway support.**」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
  - Robin sequence：「If NP airway tube treatment is not successful, then **nasal continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) can be undertaken and has been described for long-term therapy.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕→ 注意：在 Robin sequence，**CPAP 的順位在 prone positioning 與 NP airway「之後」**，不是第一線。
- **✅ 正確的部分**：「**手術是保留給保守治療失敗者**」這個原則課本完全支持——「**Respiratory distress is managed surgically when it is refractory to conservative measures.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕

**② 「兒童 OSA 第一線是 adenotonsillectomy」→ ❌ 課本查無，且與課本描述的族群不符**
- ⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查七章全文；搜過 `adenotonsillectomy`、`tonsillectomy`、`tonsil`、`adenoid`）。**全部 0 次命中。**
- ❗ **更重要的是：這兩本書談的「兒童 OSA」是 Robin sequence／CFM／syndromic craniosynostosis，其阻塞部位是 tongue base（glossoptosis）與 midfacial hypoplasia，不是扁桃腺肥大**，所以課本的第一線根本不是 adenotonsillectomy。**口試若照這句答，很可能被考官反問「你講的是哪一種小孩？」**
- **課本的兒童第一線（Robin sequence）是保守治療**：
  - 「**Respiratory distress can be managed conservatively with prone positioning and supplemental oxygen for most children with RS with isolated base-of-tongue airway obstruction. Nasopharyngeal airway may be utilized if these measures are inadequate.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
  - 「**Prone positioning is the initial non-surgical maneuver** attempted in airway management to displace the chin and tongue base forward, and can be augmented by supplemental oxygen if needed... **To be effective, the prone position must be maintained 24 hours a day, even during feeding, baths, and diaper changes.**」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
  - 「**Most patients with RS, particularly isolated RS, can be managed without surgery** but continued evaluation during this non-surgical management is critical.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕
  - NP airway 成效：「In the largest series to date, **77 patients had attempted management with NP airway alone. Of these, 63 (81.8%) had successful treatment**, with a mean hospital stay of 10 days... **The mean time until airway removal was 8 months**, as guided by PSG evaluation.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕

---

#### ✅ 課本真正的「治療先後」——請改背這三條階梯

**【階梯 A】Robin sequence（小兒）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28, Algorithm 28.1 & 28.2〕
1. **Prone positioning ± supplemental oxygen**（24 小時維持）
2. **Nasopharyngeal (NP) airway**（成功率 81.8%；平均住院 10 天；平均 8 個月後移除，以 PSG 為依據）→ **必做 split-night PSG 確認 OSA 已解除**
3. **Nasal CPAP**（NP airway 失敗時）／palatal appliance（若為單純 tongue-base 阻塞）
4. **手術**（refractory 才做）：先做 **DISE ＋ bronchoscopy** 分流 →
   - **Nonsyndromic、單純 tongue base 阻塞、無 laryngomalacia** → 可先試 **TLA（tongue–lip adhesion）**
   - **Syndromic、或下顎生長潛能差、或合併輕度 supraglottic laryngomalacia** → **MDO 優先於 TLA**
   - **Severe secondary airway lesion／MDO 失敗** → **Tracheostomy**（也是急性呼吸衰竭時的第一線緊急處置）
5. 課本原句：「Currently available studies suggest that **MDO is more effective at reducing obstructive sleep apnea and avoiding tracheostomy than TLA**.」〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch28〕

**【階梯 B】Syndromic craniosynostosis**〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
1. 新生兒期：**severe OSA → 暫時性 tracheostomy**；**mild–moderate OSA → CPAP**（可為 definitive therapy 或 bridge to surgery）
2. **Midface surgery 建議延到 5 歲以後，理想是 8–9 歲**（「midfacial advancement should not be performed under the age of 5 unless extreme situations arise」）
3. **有 OSA／tracheostomy／exorbitism** → **monobloc frontofacial advancement 或 subcranial Le Fort III**；**同時有 ICP 上升／frontal dysmorphology** → 選 **monobloc**（因其同時擴張顱內容積）
4. 課本明列 midfacial surgery 的 physiologic goal：「**increases in nasopharyngeal airway to treat OSA**」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕
5. 課後題答案（可直接背）：「The monobloc procedure... **Advancement of the midface also increases the nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal airway spaces, allowing for the treatment of obstructive sleep apnea.**」〔Grabb 9e Ch27〕

**【階梯 C】成人／骨性 OSA**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11＋Grabb 9e Ch29〕
1. 病史有 snoring／OSA → **formal sleep study 必須在擬定治療計畫前完成**〔Neligan Ch21.11〕
2. **Class II retrognathia 合併 documented severe snoring 或 OSA → 做 bimaxillary advancement（不只單顎）**〔Neligan Ch21.11〕
3. **MMA ＋ counterclockwise occlusal plane rotation**（建議 **mandible-first**）〔Neligan Ch21.11〕
4. **⚠ 絕對禁忌方向**：「**Mandibular advancement procedures increase the cross-sectional area of the airway. Conversely, mandibular setback procedures reduce cross-sectional area, meaning that conditions such as obstructive sleep apnea are relative contraindications to mandibular setback.**」〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕→ **這是 Grabb Ch29 對 OSA 最重要的一句，極易考。**
5. 「**Skeletal volume expansion is almost always preferred over constriction, particularly at the level of the airway. In patients prone to obstructive sleep symptoms, expansion of the airway volume is always desired.**」〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕
6. 生長中孩童的例外：「Rare exceptions can be made for growing patients that need an **early orthognathic surgery** to correct a severe skeletal malocclusion **or treat obstructive sleep apnea**, knowing that another jaw surgery may be needed at the time of skeletal maturity. This second orthognathic surgery... is referred to as a **"finishing osteotomy."**」〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕

---

# 四、隆乳（有植入物）的預防性抗生素　〔林育賢〕🔴 手冊沒有，且現有說法會帶錯

⚠ 手冊傷口那幾節寫的「一般乾淨傷口不需要預防性抗生素」是**裂傷**的答案。
有植入物完全相反，這一項要當成獨立主題。

Neligan Vol 5（Breast）／Grabb 的 breast augmentation 章。

### §4.1 ⭐ 給法 🟡
我的認知：術前 30–60 分鐘單劑 first-generation cephalosporin（cefazolin 2 g，體重 >120 kg 給 3 g），**術後 24 小時內停**（SCIP／ASPS 2014）。
要確認：**課本的立場是什麼？**有沒有提到實務上很多人開 5–7 天？（我查到 2009 年 ASPS 會員調查有 71.7% 開平均 5.6 天——這正是手冊要並列的那種衝突）

課本：

**⚠ 重要結論：兩本教科書「不一致」，而且 Neligan 內部各章也彼此不一致。這一題沒有單一標準答案，口試要兩邊都講。**

---

**【A】立場一：單劑術前、術後不給（Grabb 主張）**

〔Grabb 9e Ch13 Patient Safety in Plastic Surgery〕—— 抄原文：

> "Current evidence has shown that the use of antibiotic prophylaxis is beneficial in the reduction of SSI in **clean surgery of the breast**, and clean-contaminated, contaminated, and dirty plastic surgery procedures. **With the exception of breast surgery, systemic antibiotic prophylaxis is not recommended for prevention of SSI in clean plastic surgery procedures.**"
>
> "**The duration of antibiotics should be limited to a single perioperative dose administered within 30 to 60 minutes of incision time. There is no evidence to suggest that prolonged antibiotic use reduces SSIs or implant explantation rates.** However, increased duration of antibiotics has been shown to increase bacterial diversity, rates of gram-negative infections, allergic reactions, and *Clostridium difficile* colitis."
>
> "Current literature does not provide any evidence that postoperative systemic antibiotics effectively lowers the incidence of SSI associated with drains… **the presence of surgical drains is not an indication for postoperative antibiotics.**"

同章章末考題（Q2 答案 a）再確認一次：

> "Antibiotic prophylaxis is indicated in clean procedures of the breast; therefore, this patient undergoing mastopexy requires perioperative prophylaxis. **The duration of antibiotics should be limited to a single perioperative dose of cefazolin administered within 30 to 60 minutes of incision time.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch03 Primary breast augmentation with implants〕—— 作者（Randquist）自己的 protocol，抄原文：

> "During induction the patient receives **600 mg of intravenous Dalacin（clindamycin）for antibiotic prophylaxis without any additional postoperative antibiotics.**"
>
> （術後醫囑段）"The patient receives paracetamol 1 g × 4 for 6 days in combination with OxyContin 10 mg × 4 for 4 days. **No postoperative antibiotics are prescribed.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12 A critical analysis of irrigation solutions in breast surgery〕結論段：

> "**Perioperative intravenous antibiotics have been proven to limit surgical site infection.**"（只講 perioperative，未主張延長）

---

**【B】立場二：術後仍要給（Neligan 重建章的反論）**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25 Management of complications of prosthetic breast reconstruction〕—— **這段直接反駁 SCIP，抄原文**：

> "**Guidelines based on the Surgical Care Improvement Project call for withholding postoperative antibiotics in prosthetic breast reconstruction. However, adherence with these measures have been shown to increase the infection rates nearly twofold.** A systematic review similarly found that patients **without perioperative antibiotics experienced a 14.4% infection rate compared to 5.8% in those who received 24 hours of antibiotic prophylaxis**. At this point, **randomized control trial data has clearly demonstrated the benefit of postoperative antibiotics, likely for 24 hours postoperatively; another trial showed no difference between 24 hours versus until drain removal.**"

〔Grabb 9e Ch66 Breast Reconstruction—Prosthetic Techniques〕—— 承認「實務上開到拔管」但同時說沒有證據：

> "**Antibiotics are usually continued until drains are removed; however, studies have not demonstrated a correlation with extended antibiotics and decreased surgical site infections compared to short-course perioperative antibiotics alone.**"

---

**【C】⭐ 課本確實有寫「實務上開 5–7 天」的落差 —— 但數字不是 71.7%／5.6 天**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch05 Augmentation mastopexy〕—— **這是課本裡唯一明講「開 7 天」並自承與 SCIP 衝突的地方，抄原文**：

> "Unless allergic, patients are given the following postoperative medications: a narcotic with acetaminophen for pain control; a benzodiazepine as an anxiolytic and to lessen muscle spasms; and **7 days of postoperative antibiotics. According to the Surgical Care Improvement Project, antibiotics are not recommended after 24 h postoperatively and each physician must decide which protocol works best in their practice.**"

→ 口試可直接用這句：「課本自己就記載了 7 天 vs SCIP 24 小時的落差，並說由各醫師自行決定。」

---

**【D】查無的部分（不要在口試講成課本有寫）**

- ⚠ **2009 年 ASPS 會員調查「71.7%／平均 5.6 天」這組數字，課本查無明確出處。**（已查：Grabb 9e Ch13 / Ch62 / Ch66；Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch03 / Ch05 / Ch07 / Ch12 / Ch19 / Ch25；Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09；搜過關鍵字 antibiotic、prophyla、71.7、5.6、survey、duration）。Neligan Vol5 Ch12 只引用了**另一份 2018 年 ASPS 會員調查**，且該調查問的是**沖洗液偏好**不是抗生素天數：「According to a 2018 national survey of ASPS members, most surgeon respondents prefer TAS and TAS with Betadine, with only a small minority (11%) reporting the use of strictly Betadine variants.」
- ⚠ **cefazolin 2 g／體重 >120 kg 給 3 g 的體重分層劑量，課本查無明確出處。**（已查上列同一批章節；Grabb Ch13 只寫 "a single perioperative dose of cefazolin"，未給 mg 數）。課本有明確劑量的只有 Neligan Vol5 Ch03 的 **clindamycin（Dalacin）600 mg IV 誘導時單劑**。

**→ §4.1 口試講法建議：**
1. 先給共識：breast surgery 是 clean surgery 中**少數該給** prophylaxis 的例外〔Grabb Ch13〕。
2. 時機：**incision 前 30–60 分鐘**〔Grabb Ch13〕。
3. 天數：**美容隆乳偏向單劑／不給術後**〔Grabb Ch13、Neligan Vol5 Ch03〕；**植入物重建則有 RCT 支持術後 24 小時**〔Neligan Vol5 Ch25〕。
4. 明確指出落差：課本自己記載有人開 7 天、有人開到拔 drain，但**證據不支持延長**〔Neligan Vol5 Ch05、Grabb Ch66〕。

### §4.2 過敏替代與 redosing 🟡
我的認知：penicillin 過敏 → clindamycin 或 vancomycin；cefazolin 每 4 小時 redose，或失血 >1500 mL。

課本：

**【對的部分】過敏替代 —— 課本有寫，但比你的說法更嚴格**

〔Grabb 9e Ch13〕抄原文：

> "The most common cause of SSI in plastic surgery patients is ***Staphylococcus aureus*, which is adequately covered by cefazolin. For patients with beta-lactam allergies, clindamycin may be substituted. Vancomycin should only be used in patients with a history of methicillin-resistant *S. aureus* (MRSA) infection.**"

**⚠ 修正你的認知**：課本把 clindamycin 與 vancomycin **分層**，不是並列的二選一——
- β-lactam 過敏 → **clindamycin**（首選替代）
- **vancomycin 僅限有 MRSA 感染病史者**，不是單純過敏就用。

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕在**沖洗液**（非全身性）層面也提到過敏調整：

> "…one or more components may be replaced with povidone-iodine solution, vancomycin, or other antimicrobials such as chlorhexidine gluconate **based on patient allergies** and local practices."

**【查無的部分】**

- ⚠ **cefazolin 術中每 4 小時 redose 的間隔，課本查無明確出處。**（已查：Grabb 9e Ch13 Patient Safety、Ch62、Ch66、Ch75；Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09 Patient safety in plastic surgery、Vol1 Ch31；Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch03 / Ch05 / Ch07 / Ch12 / Ch25。搜過關鍵字 redos、re-dos、redose、interval、half-life、4 hours、intraoperative dose）
- ⚠ **失血 >1500 mL 即 redose 的門檻，課本查無明確出處。**（已查同上；搜過 1500、blood loss、EBL、re-dosing）
- 附帶提醒：**Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09（Patient safety in plastic surgery）整章完全沒有討論抗生素預防**，只談 VTE／Caprini、opioid、liposuction 安全、輸液。若考官問「Neligan 的 patient safety 章怎麼寫抗生素」，正確答案是**那章不談這個**，抗生素在 Grabb Ch13。

### §4.3 為什麼有植入物就不一樣 🟢
我的認知：biofilm。細菌附著在植入物表面形成生物膜，抗生素與免疫細胞穿不透 → 這是 subclinical infection 與 **capsular contracture** 的共同機轉，也是 BIA-ALCL 假說的一環。

課本：

**🟢 你的認知：完全正確。兩本教科書都這樣寫。**

**（1）biofilm → subclinical infection → capsular contracture**

〔Grabb 9e Ch62〕抄原文：

> "**The infection theory has been studied and appears currently to be the most cited explanation for capsular contracture development.** This theory entails a **chronic subclinical infectious process located adjacent to the implant shell within a microscopic biofilm that is protective of the infectious process and inaccessible to cellular and humoral immune function** to combat the inflammatory process. ***Staphylococcus epidermidis*, *Propionibacterium*, *Enterobacter*, *Bacillus*, and other organisms have been implicated in this process.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch07〕幾乎相同文字（同一作者群 Calobrace）：

> "The infection theory has been studied and appears currently to be the most cited explanation for capsular contracture development. This theory entails a chronic subclinical infectious process located adjacent to the implant shell within a microscopic biofilm that is protective of the infectious process and inaccessible to cellular and humoral immune function to combat the inflammatory process."

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕給了**機轉細節與因果證據**，抄原文：

> "**At low bacterial concentrations, the biofilm produces minimal inflammation. However, once bacterial concentration surpasses a critical density, the bacteria proliferate unchecked by the immune system, eliciting chronic inflammation, scarring, and fibrosis surrounding the implant.**"
>
> "In 1981, Burkhardt et al. and Shah et al. were the first to suggest that subclinical infection led to capsular contracture… authors found a **strong association between biofilm positivity (P < 0.0006) and the presence of *Staphylococcus epidermidis* (P < 0.01) with capsular contracture. A causal link between subclinical infection, biofilm formation, and capsular contracture was later demonstrated in an in vivo porcine model** of implant-based augmentation mammaplasty. Breast pocket inoculation of *Staphylococcus epidermidis* induced biofilm development (P = 0.0095), and **biofilm formation was associated with a fourfold increased risk of developing capsular contracture (OR = 4.1667).**"

**（2）biofilm 形成的分子步驟（可加分的細節）**〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕：

> "Biofilm production is initiated by **bacterial attachment to a surface, followed by cell-to-cell adhesion, extracellular matrix secretion, and bacterial multiplication** on the synthetic material."
> - staphylococci biofilm：cell wall-anchored adhesins 結合 **PIA**（polymer of β-1,6-linked N-acetylglucosamine），由 **ica operon** 調控。
> - **MSCRAMMs**（microbial surface components recognizing adhesive matrix molecules）與宿主蛋白作用；*S. epidermidis* 的 **SdrF** 結合 host type I collagen。

**（3）乳房是 "clean-contaminated" site（口試常追問）**〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕：

> "The breast tissue is home to a diverse endogenous flora that arises from the complex ductal system, which has led to the breast being described as a **'clean-contaminated' site**. The infection rates following breast surgery ranges from **3% to 15%**, while the infection rates after clean surgery is much lower, ranging from **1.5% to 3%**."
>
> 六大致病菌：「Six causative organisms are predominantly involved in implant-related infections: ***Staphylococcus epidermidis, Staphylococcus aureus, Escherichia coli, Pseudomonas aeruginosa, Propionibacterium acnes, and Corynebacterium* spp.**」（近年再加 *Serratia*、*Enterococcus*、*Enterobacter*、group B *Streptococcus*、*Morganella*）
>
> 乳暈周圍導管組織的**細菌量最高**：「highest quantitative bacterial counts in the concentrated ductal tissue of the **periareolar region**」

**（4）與 BIA-ALCL 的關聯假說 —— 課本明確寫成「假說」，未定論**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch11 BIA-ALCL〕抄原文（假說全貌）：

> "**A hypothesis involving the role of bacteria in the etiology of BIA-ALCL has been proposed (Fig. 11.3). In this model, colonization by bacteria of textured implants having a high surface area produces a biofilm, which, when the bacterial load exceeds a certain threshold value, leads to chronic antigen stimulation in genetically susceptible individuals. Eventually transformation occurs, leading to the emergence and proliferation of monoclonal CD30-positive ALK-negative T cells and the development of BIA-ALCL.** Dysregulation of the **JAK1/STAT3** pathway in affected cells is likely involved, and the observed process is a slow one, **typically requiring 8–12 years between implantation and BIA-ALCL diagnosis.**"
>
> **⚠ 課本自己下的但書（一定要跟著講，否則會被打）**："**Although some data support a bacterial role in the pathogenesis of BIA-ALCL, it has not achieved universal acceptance and should not be presented in isolation from other influences, such as possible genetic factors.**"
>
> 支持證據："In vitro and in vivo models showed **higher bacterial loads, with significantly more bacteria attaching to textured implants than to smooth implants** (P <0.001 and P = 0.006)… there is also an increasing incidence of this disease in association with **increasing degrees of texture and increasing surface area** of the implanted devices."
>
> 類比其他菌–淋巴瘤關係：*Helicobacter pylori* → gastric B-cell lymphoma／gastric cancer；*Coxiella burnetii*（Q-fever）→ B-cell lymphoma（SIR 25.4）；pcALCL 的 staphylococcal **superantigen** 刺激特定 Vβ family T cells。

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕補上 ***Ralstonia pickettii*** 這條線與**反證**：

> "**16 S pyrosequencing performed on BIA-ALCL and nontumor breast capsule specimens, *Ralstonia pickettii* revealed significant quantities in BIA-ALCL specimens (P < 0.05) in 10 of 19 specimens, whereas *Staphylococcus* spp. was more prevalent in capsular contracture specimens (P < 0.001).**"
>
> **反證**："**a subsequent analysis of the microbiota of breast implants, capsules, and parenchyma in the affected as well as the contralateral control breast in women with BIA-ALCL failed to identify either a Gram-negative shift or disproportionate amount of *Ralstonia* spp.** Rather, these patients were characterized by a preponderance of *Propionibacterium acnes* and *Staphylococcus epidermidis* consistent with the microbiome of the breast identified in numerous other studies."
>
> **沖洗液能不能預防 BIA-ALCL？課本說沒有臨床證據**："**To date, there have been no clinical studies on the possible benefits of antibiotic irrigation to prevent development of BIA-ALCL.** … Given that many textured implants have been removed from the market, it is unlikely that future antibiotic irrigation practices would measurably impact the incidence of BIA-ALCL."

〔Grabb 9e Ch13〕也順帶提了一句："In rare cases, periprosthetic breast implant infection may result in the development of breast implant–associated anaplastic large cell lymphoma."

### §4.4 ⭐ Pocket irrigation 與 14-point plan 🔴
我的認知：
- **Adams triple antibiotic solution**：bacitracin 50,000 U ＋ cefazolin 1 g ＋ gentamicin 80 mg in 500 mL saline
- Betadine：FDA 2000 年禁止接觸 implant，2017 年解禁，現在很多人用 10% povidone-iodine
- ⚠ **注射用 bacitracin 已被 FDA 於 2020 年退市** —— 課本是不是還寫舊配方？
- **Adams 14-point plan** 的完整條目我背不全
**這一項要抄整段。**

課本：

**主力出處：〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12 A critical analysis of irrigation solutions in breast surgery〕—— 整章就是在講這題。**

---

#### （1）🟢 Adams triple antibiotic solution（TAS）確切配方 —— **你背的完全正確**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕**抄原文**：

> "**TAS consists of 50,000 U Bacitracin, 1 g Ancef, 80 mg gentamicin in 500 cc of saline.** This combination of antibacterial agents has proven efficacious at controlling bacterial growth, including a spectrum of antibiotic-resistant organisms such as **methicillin-resistant *Staphylococcus aureus* and *Pseudomonas* spp.**"

（Ancef＝cefazolin 的商品名。）

**同章還給了兩個含 Betadine 的衍生配方，一併抄下來（考官很愛追問）**：

> "The separate components of TAS have also been combined with povidone-iodine to generate theoretically stronger antibiotic solutions:
> - **PCG solution** consists of **50 cc of 5% povidone-iodine (50% Betadine), 1 g cefazolin, 80 mg gentamicin in 500 cc of normal saline**, while
> - **BPCG solution** consists of Betadine added to TAS (**50,000 U of bacitracin, 50 cc of 5% povidone-iodine, 1 g cefazolin, 80 mg gentamicin, in 500 cc of normal saline**)."

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕從重建端描述同一套配方（未給劑量）：

> "Both the mastectomy pockets and the implants themselves are typically irrigated with solutions containing one or more antibiotics, although the efficacy of such practices is a continued topic for debate. **Typical antibiotic-containing solutions include bacitracin, gentamicin, and cefazolin**, although one or more components may be replaced with povidone-iodine solution, vancomycin, or other antimicrobials such as chlorhexidine gluconate based on patient allergies and local practices."

---

#### （2）🔴 Betadine 的 FDA 歷史 —— **抄整段**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕**抄原文**：

> "Throughout the 1990s, povidone-iodine irrigation was the antibiotic solution of choice due to its cost-effectiveness, low allergy risk, and bactericidal effects against a broad spectrum of organisms. However, **in 1997, Mentor Corporation found an association of valve-patch delamination of saline implant in vitro analyses of long-term intraluminal povidone-iodine solution fills.** This was further corroborated when a second study evaluated the effect of povidone-iodine on silicone elastomer tubing, which demonstrated deleterious changes in elastomer strength sufficient to result in rupture of the tubes on explantation. **In the spring of 2000, the US Food and Drug Administration (FDA) reacted to these reports by issuing a ban on the use of any povidone-iodine with breast implants.**"
>
> "Many groups were disinclined to adopt these rigorous FDA regulations due to lack of evidence regarding the dangers of povidone-iodine irrigation. **Zambacos et al. published an in vitro experiment that showed povidone-iodine to have no significantly different effect on the tensile strength of silicone elastomer shells after 4 weeks of incubation when compared with saline.** Other physicians continued to use povidone-iodine irrigation despite the FDA ban… **Despite the physician opposition and host of contrary evidence to the risk povidone-iodine pocket irrigation, it was not until 2017 that the FDA instituted a change in the 'directions for use' that removed the ban against povidone-iodine use in breast augmentation.**"
>
> "**During the 17-year ban, surgeons were tasked with developing novel antibiotic solutions for breast pocket irrigation. Notably, Adams et al. recommended a povidone-iodine-free triple-antibiotic solution consisting of a combination of bacitracin, cefazolin, and gentamicin, though they concluded that this solution was less effective at attacking bacterial cell membranes than its povidone-iodine-containing counterpart.**"

→ **你的認知（2000 禁、2017 解禁）完全正確，且課本補了「1997 Mentor valve-patch delamination」這個起因，以及「17 年禁令」這個標籤。**

**TAS 的歷史來源（Adams 兩篇 PRS 的關係，考官可能追）**〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕：

> "In 2000, Adams et al. investigated… They compared the efficacy of serial dilutions of povidone-iodine, gentamicin/polymyxin B, and gentamicin/cefazolin. **Povidone-iodine was the most effective, but was only bactericidal against most organisms at cytotoxic levels.** While povidone-iodine was relatively ineffective against *Pseudomonas aeruginosa*, except at 100% stock dilution, gentamicin/cefazolin exhibited strong antibiotic activity against these organisms. **Though neither solution was completely effective, they worked synergistically in combination with improved efficacy at lower dilutions.** The consensus among authors was to **irrigate with 10% povidone-iodine in gentamycin/cefazolin**… **Following the 2000 US FDA decision discouraging the use of povidone-iodine with breast implants, Adams et al. replaced the 10% povidone-iodine component with 50,000 U Bacitracin, with maintenance of similar efficacy in subsequent studies.**"

---

#### （3）🔴 現在建議的濃度與 dwell time —— **抄整段**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕**抄原文**：

> "**The standard concentration used for breast irrigation is 10% povidone-iodine (100% Betadine), but dilutions as low as 2.5% povidone-iodine have been shown to be bactericidal.**"
>
> Jewell 等人的資料："**povidone-iodine was effective against all strains with 5 min of exposure time, even at dilute concentrations (0.5% PI). The presence of serum proteins similar to those found in the breast pocket necessitated higher concentrations of povidone-iodine (2.5%) to eradicate bacteria**, although still efficacious after 5 min of exposure. **TAS alone did not exhibit significant reduction of the strains of planktonic bacteria tested. However, when combined with 10% povidone-iodine in a 1:1 ratio, bactericidal effects for all tested species were achieved with only minutes of exposure.**"
>
> **對抗 biofilm（不是浮游菌）的結論 —— 這是全章最重要的兩句**："Combined, results from this work suggest that **(a) povidone-iodine is a critical component for antimicrobial efficacy against biofilms, either alone or in combination with TAS and (b) an irrigation time of at least 5 min is required to achieve bactericidal activity against biofilm-associated microorganisms.**"
>
> **Chlorhexidine**："A solute concentration of **0.05% chlorhexidine** is recommended for breast pocket antibiotic irrigation." 但——"**Chlorhexidine solution achieved sterility for all strains with 15 min of exposure**… When evaluating these results, one must also consider the **2017 FDA warning on intra-operative allergic reactions to chlorhexidine. For this reason, chlorhexidine irrigation should not be used as a first-choice antibiotic solution until further safety and efficacy data is collected.**"
>
> **Hypochlorous acid（新選項）**：0.025% amber glass-stabilized HOCl 是唯一能在 5 分鐘內清除各種植入物表面 *Ralstonia pickettii* biofilm 的溶液（Brindle 等）；但 Hu 等的資料相反，HOCl 在含「biologic soil」的環境下無法清除 *S. aureus*。
>
> **全章對「哪一種最好」的最終判斷**："**Taken together, there remains a lack of consensus regarding the ideal solution, if any, for minimizing bacterial contamination of breast implants. Of the proposed irrigation solutions, povidone-iodine appears to have the most robust track record of eradicating breast implant-associated bacteria.**"

**Grabb 的立場（跟 Neligan 略有出入，兩個都要寫）**

〔Grabb 9e Ch62 Augmentation Mammoplasty〕**明確推薦 50% Betadine**，抄原文：

> "Current recommendations are for skin preparation with **chlorhexidine**, which covers most organisms including methicillin-resistant *Staphylococcus aureus*. Likewise, **perioperative antibiotics and pocket irrigation with 50% Betadine reduce implant contamination and possible infection.**"
>
> "Pocket irrigation with antibiotic solutions has proven beneficial in reducing the incidence of capsular contracture. **Our current recommendation is for pocket irrigation with 50% Betadine.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch07 Prevention and management of complications〕（同作者 Calobrace）：

> "**Antibiotic irrigation containing Betadine seems to provide the best coverage, either the Betadine-containing original Adam's formula or alternatively 50% Betadine.**"

〔Grabb 9e Ch66 Prosthetic Techniques〕重建術式描述：

> "Meticulous hemostasis is obtained, and **the pocket is irrigated with an antibiotic solution as well as a dilute Betadine solution.** All members of the surgical team change gloves and handling of the expander is limited to a single surgeon."

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕對 Betadine 安全性的最新表述：

> "**Previous concerns about implant shell weakening and possible rupture with the use of povidone-iodine irrigation have been largely refuted.**"

**⚠ 兩本不一致之處（口試要點出）**：
- **Grabb Ch62／Neligan Ch07（Calobrace）＝ 50% Betadine 為主**；
- **Neligan Ch12（Myckatyn 等）＝ 10% povidone-iodine（100% Betadine）為標準濃度，2.5% 已足夠殺菌，且強調 dwell ≥ 5 分鐘**。
- 濃度講法不同（50% Betadine ≈ 5% povidone-iodine；100% Betadine ＝ 10% povidone-iodine），答題時**務必說清楚是「Betadine 的百分比」還是「povidone-iodine 的百分比」**，這是最常被抓的陷阱。

---

#### （4）⚠ Adams 14-point plan 的完整條目

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處（14 個條目沒有任何一章列出來）。**

已查章節與關鍵字：Grabb 9e Ch62 Augmentation Mammoplasty／Ch66 Prosthetic Techniques／Ch13 Patient Safety／Ch75 Liposuction；Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch02 Current status of breast implants／Ch03 Primary breast augmentation／Ch05 Augmentation mastopexy／Ch07 Prevention and management of complications／Ch10 Breast implant illness／Ch11 BIA-ALCL／Ch12 Irrigation solutions／Ch19 Introduction to prosthetic reconstruction／Ch25 Complications of prosthetic reconstruction；Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09 Patient safety／Ch26 Tissue expansion and implants／Ch31 Implants and biomaterials。搜過關鍵字：`14-point`、`14 point`、`fourteen`、`Deva`、`Adams`、`plan`、`biofilm`（含去連字號正規化後再搜）。

**課本裡唯一提到它的一句**〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch07〕：

> "Box 7.1 summarizes some of the implant and surgical technique options that have been associated with lower capsular contractures. **The 14-point plan has also been proposed as a strategy for reducing the incidence of capsular contracture.**"

該句的引用文獻是 **Deva AK, Adams WP, Vickery K. *The role of bacterial biofilms in device-associated infections.* Plast Reconstr Surg. 2013;132:1319–1328.**（→ 若考官追問，正確回答是「這在 Deva/Adams 的 PRS 2013 原著，教科書只引用未列舉」）

**替代答案：課本自己列出的「降低 capsular contracture 的措施」清單 —— 這才是有出處可抄的版本。**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch07, **BOX 7.1 Associated with reduced capsular contracture incidence**〕—— **逐條抄原文**：

> - **No-touch technique**
> - **Nipple shields**
> - **Pocket irrigation with antibiotics**
> - **Insertion sleeve**
> - **Submuscular implant pocket**
> - **Textured implants**
> - **Inframammary incision**
> - **Cohesive shaped implants**

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕的「術中減少污染」清單（可與 Box 7.1 互補）：

> "These include **antibiotic irrigation of the breast pocket, as well as atraumatic pocket dissection, antibiotic soaking of implants prior to placement, glove changing before implant handling, implant insertion with a funnel, and use of nipple barriers.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch05〕的版本：

> "Intraoperative techniques such as **re-prepping the chest; changing gloves; avoiding multiple people handling the implants; placing an occlusive dressing over the nipple; using 'no-touch' technique; irrigating the pocket with antibiotic solution or Betadine solution and rinse; and avoiding powdered gloves** have all been utilized to help reduce risk."

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕再補幾條（重建端）：

> "Sterility is of utmost importance when handling the breast implant. **The sterile container housing the implant should not be opened until the pocket is completely prepared to receive it.** Even though many surgeons opt to replace their outer sterile gloves with a fresh, clean pair before handling the implant a **'no touch technique'** may further help to minimize the risk of contamination. **A randomized trial even demonstrated decreased infection rates with disposable sterile draping systems compared with reusable ones.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch03〕作者實作細節（可當口試的「我平常怎麼做」）：

> - 皮膚準備：**10% chlorhexidine solution**，範圍上至鎖骨、外至 dorsal axillary line、下至臍；**必須等它乾 3 分鐘**（"It is recommended that the prep be allowed to dry for 3 min as per the manufacturer for optimal benefit."）
> - **乳頭與切口以 Tegaderm shields 覆蓋**
> - **Double antibiotic solution（cefuroxime 1.5 g and gentamycin 80 mg）** 先注入裝 textured implant 的無菌盒中浸泡（"Pre-soaking the devices may also eradicate static electricity, thereby reducing the adhesion of airborne particles onto the implants…"）；**用 nanotextured smooth implant 時作者不用抗生素浸泡**
> - 放入前**更換手套**、**用 funnel 置入**、置入時除植入物外不碰任何東西

---

#### （5）⚠ 「注射用 bacitracin 已退市／FDA 2020 撤市」

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處。**（已查上列全部章節；搜過關鍵字 `bacitracin`、`withdraw`、`recall`、`discontinu`、`market`、`2020`、`unavailable`、`shortage`。）

**兩本教科書都仍在寫含 bacitracin 的舊配方**：
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕："TAS consists of **50,000 U Bacitracin**, 1 g Ancef, 80 mg gentamicin in 500 cc of saline."
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕："Typical antibiotic-containing solutions include **bacitracin**, gentamicin, and cefazolin."

→ **口試策略**：先照課本背 TAS 原始配方（bacitracin 50,000 U ＋ cefazolin 1 g ＋ gentamicin 80 mg／500 mL），再補一句「**注射用 bacitracin 目前在美國已不再供應，因此臨床上多改用不含 bacitracin 的 PCG solution（5% povidone-iodine 50 cc ＋ cefazolin 1 g ＋ gentamicin 80 mg／500 mL）或直接用稀釋 povidone-iodine**」——**PCG 這個替代配方是課本有寫的**〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch12〕，可安全講；但「FDA 2020 撤市」這句要說明是課本之外的資訊，不要當成課本內容。

### §4.5 牙科處置前要不要給抗生素 🟡
我的認知：AHA 不建議乳房植入物病人在牙科處置前常規給預防性抗生素（跟人工關節、心臟瓣膜不同）。這題常被當追問。

課本：

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處。**

已查章節：Grabb 9e Ch62 Augmentation Mammoplasty／Ch66 Breast Reconstruction—Prosthetic Techniques／Ch13 Patient Safety in Plastic Surgery；Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch02 Current status of breast implants／Ch03 Primary breast augmentation／Ch05 Augmentation mastopexy／Ch07 Prevention and management of complications／Ch10 Breast implant illness／Ch11 BIA-ALCL／Ch12 Irrigation solutions／Ch19 Introduction to prosthetic reconstruction／Ch25 Complications of prosthetic reconstruction；Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09 Patient safety／Ch26 Tissue expansion and implants／Ch31 Implants and biomaterials。
搜過關鍵字：`dental`、`dentist`、`dental procedure`、`endocarditis`、`AHA`、`American Heart Association`、`hematogenous`、`joint replacement`、`prosthetic joint`、`bacteremia`、`transient bacteremia`、`late infection`、`prophyla`。

**這些章節出現的 "dental" 全部與本題無關**，逐一確認：
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch11〕的 "dental" 是指**牙科植體本身引發的 ALCL 個案報告**："There have been single case reports of BIA-ALCL in association with **dental**, gastric lap band, gluteal and silicone-containing intravenous ports."（以及對應的參考文獻 37）
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch31〕與〔Vol1 Ch26〕的 "dental" 是指**牙科／顱顏用生醫材料**，與抗生素預防無關。

**→ 口試講法建議**：
1. 誠實說「**這一點兩本主要教科書的乳房章節都沒有明文規範**」，不要硬掰課本有寫。
2. 用課本**能支持的推論**回答：課本明確把 late/delayed infection 與 **biofilm、subclinical infection** 綁在一起〔Neligan Vol5 Ch12、Ch25〕，而〔Neligan 5e Vol5 Ch25〕指出「**Nearly half of all infections following prosthetic breast reconstruction present more than 30 days after the procedure**」——遲發性感染確實存在，但課本從未把它歸因於牙科菌血症，也從未建議牙科前給藥。
3. 再補「AHA 指引不建議乳房植入物病人常規牙科預防性給藥（與人工瓣膜、特定先天性心臟病不同）」，並註明**這是指引層級、非本次教科書章節內容**。

### §4.6 順帶：他的其他刀 🟡
林育賢的美容刀以眼整形、breast、liposuction 為主，另有 buccal ca. recon。
要確認：**抽脂**的安全上限（大量抽脂的定義、體液補充）——手冊有沒有？如果沒有也一起列進來。

課本：

**兩本一致：large-volume liposuction ＝ 一次手術抽出 > 5000 mL（5 L）lipoaspirate。**

---

**（1）大量抽脂的定義**

〔Grabb 9e Ch75 Liposuction, Abdominoplasty, and Belt Lipectomy〕抄原文：

> "**Large-volume liposuction is defined as >5 L of lipoaspirate.**"
>
> "**Large-volume liposuction, commonly defined as a lipoaspirate volume of 5 L or more, warrants additional safety guidelines.**"

〔Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09 Patient safety in plastic surgery〕抄原文：

> "**Large-volume liposuction is defined as removal of >5000 mL of aspirate in a single setting, although some states place lower limits on acceptable volumes.**"

---

**（2）🔴 體液補充與監測原則 —— 抄整段**

〔Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09〕**抄原文**（這句最完整，含機轉）：

> "**Current American Society of Plastic Surgeons recommendations suggest that large-volume liposuction be performed only in the hospital setting or an accredited/licensed facility, with postoperative admission to monitor for both dehydration and fluid overload as fluid transitions from the extra- to intravascular space.**"

〔Grabb 9e Ch75〕**抄原文**：

> "**Typically these procedures should be performed in a hospital setting as opposed to an outpatient surgery center with intravenous fluid replacement and possibly overnight monitoring.** For deep vein thrombosis (DVT) prevention, **pneumatic compression devices are routinely used. Chemoprophylaxis with unfractionated or low-molecular-weight heparin is considered for high-risk patients.**"
>
> 術前評估："**Because large-volume liposuction leads to greater electrolyte and hemodynamic changes, preoperative chemistry and complete blood count should be considered.**"
>
> 併發症："**Large-volume liposuction can lead to intravascular volume loss with hypotension and tachycardia if adequate fluid resuscitation has not been performed.**"

**⚠ 注意**：課本**沒有**給「每抽 1 mL 補幾 mL」的公式化補液比例（如 superwet ratio 之外的 crystalloid 換算），只給上述原則。若被追問公式，據實說課本只給原則。

---

**（3）Wetting solution 的種類與失血量**〔Grabb 9e Ch75, TABLE 75.1〕—— **抄整表**：

| Technique | Infiltrate Volume | Estimated Blood Loss（% of aspirate volume） |
|---|---|---|
| **Dry** | No infiltrate | **~20–40%** |
| **Wet** | 200–300 mL/area | **~25%** |
| **Superwet** | **1 mL infiltrate : 1 mL aspirate** | **~1–4%** |
| **Tumescent** | **To skin turgor, 2–3 mL infiltrate : 1 mL aspirate** | **~1%** |

> "**Most procedures use a superwet or tumescent solution to keep blood loss approximately 1% of the lipoaspirate volume.**"
>
> 章末考題（Q2）補充："**A dry technique is virtually never used as the blood loss is more than desired.**"

---

**（4）Lidocaine 安全劑量與毒性（口試最常接著問）**〔Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09〕**抄原文**：

> "**For liposuction, lidocaine can be dosed at up to 35 mg/kg, when paired with epinephrine.**"
>
> 藥動學："…the slow absorption of lidocaine from the fat (a poorly vascularized area) – **when epinephrine is used, plasma lidocaine levels will peak ~12 hours after initial infiltration.** For more vascular areas like the **head and neck**, it is important to understand lidocaine absorption is more rapid and **levels peak ~6 hours after infiltration** – this is important for multi-site liposuction procedures."
>
> 毒性分層："**initial symptoms of lidocaine toxicity can occur at plasma levels of 3–6 μg/mL** (light-headedness, tinnitus, slurred speech, metallic taste, lip/tongue numbness), with progressive cardiac and neurologic symptoms at increased plasma levels. **Concentrations above 10 μg/mL are associated with CNS depression** can occur, and **above 20 μg/mL, both respiratory depression and cardiac arrest can occur.**"
>
> 降低風險："**Risk of liposuction toxicity can be decreased in higher-volume cases by decreasing lidocaine concentration in the wetting solution, or using a superwet, as opposed to tumescent, infiltration.**"

---

**（5）其他安全上限（可能被一起問）**

〔Grabb 9e Ch75〕**合併手術時間上限**：

> "Combined surgery may be considered and is **typically limited to approximately 7 hours duration.**"（belt lipectomy 同樣：**"should generally not last more than 7 hours"**）

〔Grabb 9e Ch75〕**Zones of adherence（抽脂要避開，易生 contour irregularity）**：

> "**the lateral gluteal depression, the gluteal crease, the distal posterior thigh, middle medial thigh, and inferolateral iliotibial tract.**" —— 其中 **gluteal crease 完全避開**以保留臀形（"The gluteal crease is completely avoided to preserve buttock shape."）
> 層次原則："liposuction is **liberally performed in the deep layer but more cautiously in the superficial layer** to help avoid contour irregularity."

〔Neligan 5e Vol1 Ch09〕**Gluteal fat grafting 的死亡率（考官很愛問的延伸）**：

> "**mortality rates are higher than any other aesthetic procedure. Published mortality rates are between 1:2351 and 1:6241.** Pulmonary fat embolism occurs when grafted fat is inadvertently placed intravascularly… **Current care paradigms support subcutaneous fat injection only, with use of a constantly moving single hole ≥4.1 mm cannula.**"

〔Grabb 9e Ch75〕**Abdominoplasty 的抗生素**（與 §4.1 呼應）：

> "**One dose of intravenous antibiotics is given**, and the patient is warmed with an underbody warmer or Bair Huggers…"

---

---

# 五、高壓氧治療　〔潘信誠〕🟠 手冊只有「輔助手段」四個字

Neligan Vol 1（Principles）的 wound healing 相關章／Vol 4。

> **查證範圍聲明（重要，請先讀）**
> Grabb & Smith 9e 與 Neligan 5e **都沒有高壓氧專章**。本次已逐章讀取／全文檢索以下章節，
> 關鍵字：`hyperbaric`、`HBO`、`ATA`、`Marx`、`osteoradionecrosis`、`UHMS`、`Undersea`、`Wagner`、`barotrauma`、`oxygen toxicity`、`carbon monoxide`：
>
> **Grabb 9e**：Ch2 Basic Science of Wound Healing and Management of Chronic Wounds／Ch18 Thermal, Chemical, and Electrical Injuries／Ch20 Radiation and Radiation Injury／Ch39 Nasal Reconstruction／Ch47 Dermal and Soft-Tissue Fillers／Ch49 Fat Grafting／Ch110 Lower Extremity, Foot, and Ankle Reconstruction／Ch113 Pressure Injury Management and Reconstruction
> **Neligan 5e Vol 1**：Ch15 Wound healing／Ch20 Repair and grafting of bone／Ch27 Principles of radiation therapy
> **Neligan 5e Vol 4**：Ch05 Lower extremity coverage／Ch08 Foot reconstruction／Ch09.1 Diabetic foot: introduction／Ch09.2 Diabetic foot: management of wounds／Ch09.3 Diabetic foot: vascularity & soft-tissue reconstruction／Ch11 Reconstruction of the chest／Ch18 Burn, chemical, and electrical injuries／Ch03.6 Debulking: excision
>
> 另以 Google Drive 全文檢索確認：**兩本書的章節資料夾中，含 "hyperbaric" 字串的章節就只有上列這些**（Grabb：Ch2、Ch20、Ch39、Ch47、Ch49、Ch110；Neligan Vol1：Ch15、Ch20、Ch27；Vol4：Ch03.6、Ch05、Ch08、Ch09.1、Ch09.2、Ch11、Ch18）。
> **`UHMS`／`Undersea`／`ATA`／`barotrauma`／`oxygen toxicity` 在兩本書全部章節中一次都沒有出現。**

---

### §5.1 ⭐ UHMS 核可適應症 🟡
我的認知：共 14 項，整外相關的有——
crush injury／compartment syndrome／acute traumatic ischemia、compromised grafts and flaps、
delayed radiation injury（軟組織與骨壞死）、chronic refractory osteomyelitis、
diabetic foot ulcer（Wagner grade 3 以上）、necrotizing soft tissue infection、
gas gangrene、CO poisoning、arterial gas embolism、decompression sickness、
severe anemia、intracranial abscess、thermal burns、idiopathic sudden SNHL
要確認：**課本列幾項？糖尿病足的 Wagner 分級門檻是 3 還是 2？**

課本：

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處：兩本書都沒有列出 UHMS 核可適應症清單，也沒有任何「共 N 項」的條列。**
（已查：Grabb Ch2／Ch18／Ch20／Ch110／Ch113；Neligan Vol1 Ch15／Ch27；Vol4 Ch05／Ch08／Ch09.1／Ch09.2／Ch18。關鍵字 `UHMS`、`Undersea`、`Undersea and Hyperbaric Medical Society` 在兩本書中皆為 0 命中。)

**「共 14 項」是學會／UHMS 官方文件的內容，不是這兩本教科書的內容 —— 口試若被追問「課本哪裡寫」，要誠實說是 UHMS guideline，不要說是 Grabb／Neligan。**

**課本實際有寫到的 HBO 適應情境（散在各章，逐條抄錄）：**

1. **慢性難癒傷口（chronic wounds, CW）—— Grabb 9e Ch2**
   > "HBOT is recommended for most severe and recalcitrant CW with limited therapeutic alternatives."
   MCQ 解答段（Ch2, Answer 3）更明確：
   > "Hyperbaric oxygen therapy is recommended as a **second-line treatment for recalcitrant wounds not responding to initial care**."

2. **複雜非癒合傷口／放射線傷口／凍傷／再灌流後的壞疽足／瀕危皮瓣 —— Neligan Vol4 Ch08（Foot reconstruction）**
   > "level-one evidence has demonstrated the ability of systemic hyperbaric oxygen therapy (HBOT) to reduce amputation rates and promote granulation of certain complicated, non-healing wounds. It is particularly useful in **radiation-induced wounds, frostbite, revascularized gangrenous feet, and failing flaps**."
   ※ 這是兩本書中「適應症」寫得最像清單的一句，但只有 4 項，且非 UHMS 架構。

3. **糖尿病足潰瘍（DFU）—— Neligan Vol4 Ch09.2**
   > "Hyperbaric oxygen therapy (HBO) has been demonstrated to be effective as an adjunctive therapy in **some** chronic wound management. This wound care modality should be used **as a supplement in conjunction with other wound care modalities** and specifically may be beneficial in patients with **microvascular disease causing local wound ischemia**."
   同章附警語：
   > "Regardless of the physiology of HBO, **oxygenation will not reach the wound tissues if peripheral vascular occlusion exists**. A careful assessment of the **cost–benefit ratio** should be conducted prior to use and only ever in a **state-sanctioned HBO facility with properly licensed HBO specialists**."

4. **糖尿病足（IWGDF 2019 指引）—— Neligan Vol4 Ch09.1, Table 9.1.1（Wound healing 章節）**
   > "Consider negative pressure wound therapy and **hyperbaric oxygen therapy**"
   同章亦把 **hyperbaric medicine** 列為糖尿病足多專科團隊（multidisciplinary limb salvage team）的成員科別之一。

5. **氣性壞疽（gas gangrene）—— Neligan Vol4 Ch08**
   > "Adjunctive hyperbaric oxygen therapy may help in controlling the anaerobic infection **but is unnecessary if the initial debridement was adequate**."
   ※ 課本立場：**清創足夠就不需要 HBO**。這是很好的口試答題角度。

6. **一氧化碳中毒（CO poisoning）—— Neligan Vol4 Ch18（見 §5.2 詳抄）**
   ※ 這是兩本書中**唯一寫出明確數值門檻**的 HBO 適應症。

7. **填充物血管阻塞（filler-induced vascular compromise）—— Grabb 9e Ch47（Fillers）**
   處置順序第 3 項：
   > "3. **Consider hyperbaric oxygen chambers to enhance wound healing.**"（前面是 hyaluronidase + massage、topical nitroglycerin；後面是 warm compresses）

8. **移植床／植皮輔助 —— Grabb 9e Ch39（Nasal Reconstruction）、Ch49（Fat Grafting）**
   - Ch39（composite graft）：> "Adjunctive therapies to improve postoperative graft survival have been proposed, including perioperative steroids, **hyperbaric oxygen**, and postoperative cooling; **however, further research is required**."
   - Ch49（fat graft recipient site preparation）：> "Such techniques include **hyperbaric oxygen**, external or internal volume pre-expansion, pretunneling and scar release..."

9. **淋巴水腫 Charles procedure 植皮 —— Neligan Vol4 Ch03.6**
   > "**Hyperbaric oxygen may be helpful when the tissue bed is indurated with suboptimal blood supply or in the situation of peripheral arterial occlusive disease.**"

10. **下肢傷口治療流程圖 —— Neligan Vol4 Ch05（algorithm 方塊）、Vol4 Ch09.2（Fig. 演算法）、Neligan Vol1 Ch15（Algorithm 15.2 DFU）**
    HBOT 均被放在「保守／輔助治療」欄位（與 NPWT、growth factors、skin substitutes 並列），**沒有文字說明**。

---

#### ⭐ Wagner 分級門檻（考官最可能追問）

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處：兩本書都沒有寫「Wagner grade ≥3（或 ≥2）才給 HBO」這個門檻。**
（已查：Grabb Ch2／Ch110；Neligan Vol1 Ch15；Vol4 Ch08／Ch09.1／Ch09.2／Ch09.3。`Wagner` 全文只有 2 個命中，且都不是門檻。）

實際命中內容：

- **Grabb 9e Ch2**（Assessment and staging 段）把 Wagner 歸類為**歷史沿用**的分類，且明講未經驗證：
  > "Other **historically commonly used** classification systems include the Fontaine and Rutherford classifications, the **Wagner-Meggitt classification (mostly based on ABI values)**, and the University of Texas Health Science Center San Antonio diabetic wound classification..."
  > "Yet, **no high-level studies have validated the reliability of these tools.**"
- **Neligan Vol4 Ch08**：`Wagner` 只出現在參考文獻 112（Wagner FW Jr. *Amputations of the foot and ankle*. Clin Orthop Relat Res. 1977），**本文完全沒有用 Wagner 分級**。
- **Neligan Vol4 Ch09.1**（Table 9.1.1, Classification 欄）現代課本改用的是：
  - 溝通用：**SINBAD**
  - 感染評估：**IDSA/IWGDF**
  - 灌流評估：**WIfI（Wound, Ischemia, foot Infection）**
  - 族群結果稽核：**SINBAD**
  - **完全沒有 Wagner。**

**👉 口試建議講法**：「Wagner grade 3 以上」是 **UHMS 給付／適應症文件與健保給付條件**的寫法，**不是 Grabb／Neligan 的寫法**；現行 Neligan 5e 用的是 SINBAD／WIfI／IDSA-IWGDF，Grabb 9e 更直接說 Wagner-Meggitt 是歷史用法且未被高階研究驗證。這樣答比背 grade 3 更安全，也更「現代化」。

---

### §5.2 治療參數 🔴
我的認知：2.0–2.5 ATA、每次 90–120 分鐘；放射性壞死常用 **Marx protocol 30 次 ＋ 術後 10 次**。
**次數與壓力要抄。**

課本：

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處：兩本教科書全部章節中，「ATA」「壓力值」「單次治療分鐘數」「總療程次數」一個都沒有。**
（已查：Grabb Ch2／Ch18／Ch20／Ch39／Ch47／Ch49／Ch110／Ch113；Neligan Vol1 Ch15／Ch20／Ch27；Vol4 Ch03.6／Ch05／Ch08／Ch09.1／Ch09.2／Ch11／Ch18。關鍵字 `ATA` 命中數 0。）

**⚠ Marx protocol（術前 30 次＋術後 10 次）：課本查無。**
（已查：Grabb Ch20 Radiation and Radiation Injury 全章、Neligan Vol1 Ch27 Principles of radiation therapy 全章、Neligan Vol1 Ch20 Repair and grafting of bone、Neligan Vol4 Ch11 Reconstruction of the chest。）

- 「Marx」在兩本書中的**唯一命中**是 Neligan Vol1 Ch20 的**參考文獻 428**：
  > Marx RE. *Clinical application of bone biology to mandibular and maxillary reconstruction.*
  —— 這是骨生物學的文獻，**不是 HBO 30+10 的 protocol**。
- **Grabb 9e Ch20 是最可能寫 Marx protocol 的章節，但它反而給了否定的立場**（見 §5.5）。
- Neligan Vol1 Ch27 的 ORN 段落（"Bony injury"）**通篇沒有提到 hyperbaric oxygen**；`hyperbaric` 只出現在參考文獻 102 的**標題**中：
  > Clayman L. Clinical controversies in oral and maxillofacial surgery: Part two. Management of dental extractions in irradiated jaws: **a protocol without hyperbaric oxygen therapy**. J Oral Maxillofac Surg. 1997.
  —— 課本引用的是一篇「**不用 HBO** 的拔牙處置流程」。

**👉 兩本書中唯一有明確「數值」的 HBO 段落：CO 中毒（Neligan Vol4 Ch18）**（🔴 原文照抄）

> "**Hyperbaric oxygen therapy can reduce the blood half-life of carbon monoxide to 23 minutes.** If readily available, hyperbaric oxygen is recommended for patients with **carboxyhemoglobin levels greater than 25%**, **myocardial ischemia**, **cardiac dysrhythmias**, or **central neurological abnormalities**. Hyperbaric oxygen is also recommended by some for **pregnant women and young children with carboxyhemoglobin levels of ≥15%** to prevent **delayed neurological sequelae**."

同段落的常氧治療對照（同章）：
> "Treatment is with **100% oxygen with a non-rebreather facemask**, which displaces carbon monoxide from hemoglobin much faster than 21% room air oxygen."
> "Patients with **CO-HgB levels greater than 25%** have had clinically significant exposure to combustion products and **should be ventilated**."
> （半衰期：室內空氣約數小時；FIO₂=100% 時約 1 小時等級 —— 原文此處 PDF 排版錯亂，僅可確認 "The half-life of carboxyhemoglobin… ≈1 hour… FIO₂ near 100%"，**建議不要在口試引用此句的精確數字**。）
> Table 18.4（Clinical signs of carboxyhemoglobinemia）：
> - 0–10%：Minimal（重度吸菸者的正常值）
> - 10–20%：Nausea, headache
> - 20–30%：Drowsiness, lethargy
> - 30–40%：Confusion, agitation
> - 40–50%：Coma, respiratory depression
> - >50%：Death

**⚠ 注意：Grabb 9e Ch18（Thermal, Chemical, and Electrical Injuries）完全沒有寫 CO 中毒的 HBO 治療**，"Carbon monoxide poisoning" 只出現一次，是燒傷中心轉診條件表格的一列。CO 中毒的 HBO 內容**只能引 Neligan Vol4 Ch18**。

**👉 口試講法**：2.0–2.5 ATA、90–120 分鐘、Marx 30+10 都是**臨床上一般這樣操作／UHMS 與 Marx 原始文獻的內容**，請明確標示「這不是 Grabb／Neligan 的文字」。若考官逼問課本，唯一能引的數值是 CO 中毒的 COHb >25%／≥15%（孕婦與幼童）與半衰期 23 分鐘（Neligan Vol4 Ch18）。

---

### §5.3 機轉 🟢
我的認知：hyperoxygenation → 提高組織氧分壓；促進 **angiogenesis／neovascularization**；
刺激 fibroblast 增生與膠原合成；恢復白血球的 oxidative killing；抑制厭氧菌；
減少 ischemia-reperfusion injury；vasoconstriction 降低水腫。

課本：

**逐項對答（🟢 只判對／錯）**

| 你的機轉 | 判定 | 課本依據 |
|---|---|---|
| **Hyperoxygenation → 提高組織氧分壓** | ✅ **對** | Grabb Ch2：「HBOT adopts enriched oxygen in a pressurized environment... **Supersaturation of oxygenated blood**」；Neligan Vol1 Ch15：「the patient breathes 100% oxygen inside a pressurized chamber」 |
| **促進 angiogenesis／neovascularization** | ✅ **對** | Neligan Vol4 Ch08：「HBOT **stimulates local angiogenesis in the wound bed**」；Neligan Vol1 Ch15：「Oxygen tension regulates key wound healing actions, including **angiogenesis**...」 |
| **刺激 fibroblast 增生與膠原合成** | ✅ **對**（課本寫法略不同） | Neligan Vol1 Ch15：「Oxygen tension regulates key wound healing actions, including angiogenesis, **collagen synthesis**, production of growth factors... ensuring efficient leucocyte and **fibroblast function**」；Neligan Vol4 Ch08：「promotes **collagen cross-linking and extrusion from cells**」<br>※ 課本用的是 **collagen cross-linking / extrusion** 與 **fibroblast function**，不是「fibroblast 增生」，口試建議照課本用詞。 |
| **恢復白血球的 oxidative killing** | ✅ **對** | Grabb Ch2：「**increased neutrophil bactericidal activity through altered redox state**」；Neligan Vol4 Ch08：「**potentiates the ability of macrophages/granulocytes to kill bacteria**」；Neligan Vol1 Ch15：「ensuring efficient **leucocyte** ... function」 |
| **抑制厭氧菌** | 🔶 **部分對（課本只在氣性壞疽的脈絡下間接支持，沒有寫成一般機轉）** | Neligan Vol4 Ch08（氣性壞疽）：「Adjunctive hyperbaric oxygen therapy may help in **controlling the anaerobic infection** but is unnecessary if the initial debridement was adequate」<br>⚠ 兩本書都**沒有**把「抑制厭氧菌」列進 HBO 的機轉清單。 |
| **減少 ischemia-reperfusion injury** | ❌ **課本查無** | 已查全部上列章節，兩本書**均未提到 HBO 與 ischemia-reperfusion injury**。這是 UHMS／基礎生理教材的說法。 |
| **Vasoconstriction 降低水腫** | ❌ **與課本相反，請務必修正** | **Grabb 9e Ch2 原文寫的是 vasoDILATION**：<br>🔴「Supersaturation of oxygenated blood, **local vasodilation**, increased neutrophil bactericidal activity through altered redox state, modulation of inflammatory cytokines, and **reduction of edema** are supposed to contribute to healing.」<br>👉 **「減少水腫」課本有；但課本歸因於 local vasodilation，不是 vasoconstriction。** 口試若答 vasoconstriction 而考官手上是 Grabb 9e，會被抓。安全講法：「課本（Grabb 9e Ch2）列的是 supersaturation、local vasodilation、增強嗜中性球殺菌、調節發炎細胞激素、減少水腫；傳統高壓氧生理學另有 hyperoxic vasoconstriction 降低水腫的說法。」 |

**課本有、你的清單沒有的機轉（要補進去）**

1. **Modulation of inflammatory cytokines（調節發炎細胞激素）** —— Grabb Ch2。
2. **Production of growth factors（促生長因子生成）** —— Neligan Vol1 Ch15。
3. **Generation of reactive oxygen species (ROS)** —— Neligan Vol1 Ch15 明列 ROS 生成為 oxygen tension 調控的關鍵作用之一（正向而非毒性脈絡）。
4. **Collagen cross-linking and extrusion from cells** —— Neligan Vol4 Ch08（比「膠原合成」更精確）。
5. **前提條件（考官最愛的陷阱）** —— Neligan Vol4 Ch09.2：
   🔴「**Regardless of the physiology of HBO, oxygenation will not reach the wound tissues if peripheral vascular occlusion exists.**」
   👉 **有周邊血管阻塞就先 revascularization，否則 HBO 無效** —— 這句一定要背。

**Topical oxygen therapy（TOWT）—— 課本有，常被拿來當延伸題**
- Grabb Ch2：「Topical oxygen therapy also increases oxygen concentration within CW, yet **more research is needed** to validate its efficacy and to identify optimal indications.」
- Neligan Vol1 Ch15：「TOWT increases oxygen levels to a **depth of 2 mm** within the wound bed... The benefits of TOWT, **like HBOT, are equivocal**. Although TOWT may provide slight benefit in wound closure rates, **rates of amputation remain similar**.」

---

### §5.4 禁忌症與併發症 🟡
我的認知：
- 絕對禁忌：**未處理的氣胸**
- 相對：bleomycin／doxorubicin／cisplatin、COPD 併 CO₂ 滯留、幽閉恐懼、上呼吸道感染、癲癇、懷孕
- 併發症：barotrauma（**中耳最常見**）、氧中毒（CNS 抽搐、肺）、暫時性近視加深、白內障

課本：

**⚠ 課本查無明確出處：Grabb & Smith 9e 與 Neligan 5e 兩本書，在已查的全部章節中，都沒有列出高壓氧的絕對禁忌症、相對禁忌症，或併發症。**

（已查：Grabb Ch2／Ch18／Ch20／Ch39／Ch47／Ch49／Ch110／Ch113；Neligan Vol1 Ch15／Ch20／Ch27；Vol4 Ch03.6／Ch05／Ch08／Ch09.1／Ch09.2／Ch09.3／Ch11／Ch18。
關鍵字命中數：`barotrauma` = **0**、`oxygen toxicity` = **0**、`pneumothorax`（HBO 脈絡下）= **0**、`bleomycin`／`claustrophobia`／`cataract`／`myopia`（HBO 脈絡下）= **0**。）

**課本唯一接近「使用限制」的敘述（全部抄錄，只有 3 句）：**

1. **Neligan Vol4 Ch09.2**（最實用的一句，等同「相對禁忌／無效條件」）
   🔴「Regardless of the physiology of HBO, **oxygenation will not reach the wound tissues if peripheral vascular occlusion exists**. A careful assessment of the **cost–benefit ratio** should be conducted prior to use, and **only ever in a state-sanctioned HBO facility with properly licensed HBO specialists**.」
   👉 課本強調三件事：**(a) 周邊血管阻塞未解決＝無效；(b) 成本效益要評估；(c) 必須在合格高壓氧機構、由合格高壓氧專科人員執行。**

2. **Grabb 9e Ch2**（成本／證據面的限制）
   「Despite widespread use, **high-level evidence supporting its efficacy is lacking**.」

3. **Neligan Vol1 Ch15**（成本面的限制）
   「Given the **high cost** of oxygen therapy, clinical studies regarding its effectiveness and understanding which patients may benefit from HBOT or TOWT are necessary.」

**👉 口試建議講法**：你列的絕對／相對禁忌與併發症（未處理氣胸、bleomycin、COPD 併 CO₂ 滯留、幽閉恐懼、上呼吸道感染、癲癇、懷孕；中耳 barotrauma 最常見、CNS／肺氧中毒、暫時性近視、白內障）**在臨床與高壓氧醫學專科教材上是標準答案，但 Grabb 9e／Neligan 5e 兩本書並未收錄**。答題時可以講，但要標示為「臨床上一般這樣處理／高壓氧醫學專科的標準教材」，並補上課本真正強調的那三點（PAOD 未解決則無效／cost-benefit／須合格機構執行）—— 這反而是「有課本依據」的加分點，尤其潘信誠考官本身是高壓氧專家。

---

### §5.5 ⭐ 爭議：糖尿病足的證據 🔴
潘信誠是高壓氧專家，**一定會被追問證據強度**。
我的認知：近年 RCT（如 DAMOCLES）對糖尿病足的效益是**陰性或中性**的，跟教科書列為適應症有落差。
要確認：**課本怎麼寫這件事？**如果課本沒提，就照手冊慣例標成「臨床上一般這樣處理」。

課本：

**先講結論：課本「有」討論爭議，而且立場偏保留 —— 但兩本書內部彼此矛盾，且 DAMOCLES 完全沒被提到。**

#### （A）DAMOCLES trial：**⚠ 課本查無明確出處**
（已查：Grabb Ch2／Ch110；Neligan Vol1 Ch15；Vol4 Ch08／Ch09.1／Ch09.2／Ch09.3。關鍵字 `DAMOCLES` 命中數 = **0**。）
**兩本書都沒有提到 DAMOCLES trial。** 口試可以講，但必須標示為「近年 RCT 文獻，非課本內容」。

#### （B）Grabb & Smith 9e Ch2 —— 明確表態「高階證據不足」🔴 原文照抄

> "HBOT: HBOT adopts enriched oxygen in a pressurized environment to facilitate healing. Supersaturation of oxygenated blood, local vasodilation, increased neutrophil bactericidal activity through altered redox state, modulation of inflammatory cytokines, and reduction of edema are supposed to contribute to healing. **Despite widespread use, high-level evidence supporting its efficacy is lacking. HBOT is recommended for most severe and recalcitrant CW with limited therapeutic alternatives.**"

同章 MCQ 解答（Answer 3）進一步定位為**第二線**：
> "**Hyperbaric oxygen therapy is recommended as a second-line treatment for recalcitrant wounds not responding to initial care**; in addition, **it would not remove the eschar**."
（該題正解是門診非手術清創＋敷料，HBOT 是被刻意設計的錯誤選項。）

Grabb Ch2 引用的證據來源是 **Cochrane**：
> Kranke P, et al. **Hyperbaric oxygen therapy for chronic wounds.** *Cochrane Database Syst Rev.* 2015;2015(6):CD004123.

#### （C）Neligan 5e Vol 1 Ch15（Wound healing）—— 直接寫出「試驗結果不一致」與「效益 marginal」🔴 原文照抄

> "In the setting of DFUs, **the use of adjunct therapies remains unclear**. Hyperbaric oxygen therapy (HBOT) has been suggested as a treatment for DFUs, and **several clinical trials have demonstrated improved wound healing rate and a decrease in amputation rate with HBOT.¹²⁶ However, other trials suggest no improvement¹²⁷ in either metric with HBOT therapy.**"

> "With regards to wound healing, HBOT has been most carefully evaluated for the treatment of DFU. **HBOT may be indicated for a specific subset of patients with DFU but gains in wound closure rates are marginal compared to dressing therapy alone.**"

> "Given the **high cost** of oxygen therapy, clinical studies regarding its effectiveness and **understanding which patients may benefit from HBOT or TOWT are necessary**."

Neligan Vol1 Ch15 引用的正／反兩方文獻（可直接背來當「證據強度」的答案骨架）：
- 正方 ref 126：Stoekenbroek/…「Hyperbaric oxygen for the treatment of diabetic foot ulcers: a **systematic review**」*Eur J Vasc Endovasc Surg.* 2014;47(6):647–655.
- **反方 ref 127：Margolis DJ, Gupta J, Hoffstad O, et al. "Lack of effectiveness of hyperbaric oxygen therapy for the treatment of diabetic foot ulcer and the prevention of amputation: a cohort study." *Diabetes Care.* 2013;36(7):1961–1966.**
- ref 167：「Hyperbaric oxygen therapy for chronic diabetic foot ulcers: an **overview of systematic reviews**」*Diabetes Res Clin Pract.* 2021;176:108862.

**👉 這一段是全部教科書中最直接回答「證據強度」的段落 —— 建議整段背下來。**

#### （D）⚠ 課本內部衝突（考官若拿不同章節問你，要能化解）

| 章節 | 立場 | 原文 |
|---|---|---|
| **Grabb 9e Ch2** | **保留** | "high-level evidence supporting its efficacy is **lacking**" |
| **Neligan Vol1 Ch15** | **保留** | "gains in wound closure rates are **marginal** compared to dressing therapy alone" |
| **Neligan Vol4 Ch09.2** | **中性偏正** | "effective as an adjunctive therapy in **some** chronic wound management"（引文皆為 1992 Doctor／1996 Faglia／2003 Abidia 的舊 RCT） |
| **Neligan Vol4 Ch08** | ⚠ **正向，且用詞最強** | "**level-one evidence** has demonstrated the ability of systemic HBOT to **reduce amputation rates** and promote granulation of certain complicated, non-healing wounds"（引 Cruz D, Oliveira-Pinto J, Mansilha A. *The role of hyperbaric oxygen therapy in the treatment of diabetic foot ulcers: a systematic review with meta-analysis of RCTs*） |

**裁決建議（口試講法）**：
「Neligan 內部本身不一致 —— Vol4 Ch08（Foot reconstruction）沿用較舊的 meta-analysis 寫成 level-one evidence 可降低截肢率；但 Vol1 Ch15（Wound healing，較新的整合章）與 Grabb 9e Ch2 都明確指出高階證據不足、效益 marginal，並引用 Margolis 2013 的陰性 cohort 與 2015 Cochrane。**現代立場應採 Grabb Ch2 與 Neligan Vol1 Ch15：HBOT 定位為第二線、輔助性治療，用於清創／減壓／血流重建／感染控制都做足後仍不癒合的難治性傷口，並須先排除未矯正的周邊血管阻塞。**近年更大型 RCT（含 DAMOCLES）為陰性／中性結果，與此保留立場一致 —— 但這部分屬課本外文獻。」

#### （E）放射性傷害／ORN 的證據（同樣是爭議點，考官很可能連著問）🔴 原文照抄

- **Grabb 9e Ch20（Radiation and Radiation Injury）—— 對 ORN 明確否定**
  > "**Historic studies on hyperbaric oxygen have not demonstrated much benefit in the treatment of ORN**, but recent studies have expanded its application on **soft tissue recovery** following radiotherapy including breast cancer."
  引用：Meier EL, et al. *Hyperbaric oxygen therapy for local late radiation toxicity in breast cancer patients: a systematic review.* Breast. 2023;67:46-54.／Teguh DN, et al. Radiat Oncol. 2016;11(1):130.／Helmers R, et al. *The impact of hyperbaric oxygen therapy on late irradiation injury in oral microcirculation.* Head Neck. 2022.
  同章 MCQ 解答（Answer 1，ORN 情境）：
  > "While a course of antibiotics or **hyperbaric oxygen may have some benefits**, the patient needs to have imaging performed to confirm there is no evidence for recurrent disease."（正解是 CT，不是 HBO。）
  同章對 ORN 的**確定性治療**寫的是：
  > "In the setting of ORN, **definitive treatment requires aggressive debridement and reconstruction with vascularized tissue**... treatment mandates a segmental mandibulectomy and reconstruction with vascularized bone."（free fibula 為 workhorse flap）

- **Neligan Vol4 Ch11（Reconstruction of the chest）—— 同樣否定**
  > "**Hyperbaric oxygen (HBO) therapy has been trialed in managing osteoradionecrosis in other areas of the body with limited success.** These wounds must be treated with **radical resection of the involved tissue**..."
  引用的正是那篇經典陰性 RCT：**Annane D, et al. "Hyperbaric oxygen therapy for radionecrosis of the jaw: a randomized, placebo-controlled, double-blind trial from the ORN96 study group." *J Clin Oncol.* 2004;22(24):4893–4900.**

- **Neligan Vol1 Ch27（Principles of radiation therapy）—— ORN 段落完全沒提 HBO**
  ORN 的處理重點放在**預防**：放療前 dental clearance、必要拔牙在放療前完成、放療計畫時 delineate mandible/maxilla/salivary glands 並設 dose constraint、放療後終生每日 fluoride 與嚴格口腔衛生。
  `hyperbaric` 只出現在參考文獻 102 標題：「Management of dental extractions in irradiated jaws: **a protocol without hyperbaric oxygen therapy**」。
  另附 ORN 流行病學（可背）：> "ORN in the head and neck region had an incidence of **6.8% in 1968**, it is fortunately now quite rare, with more recent series reporting incidences of **0–2%**."（歸因於 dental care 與 IMRT 劑量分佈改善）
  ORN 定義與危險因子（Vol1 Ch27）：> 照射骨的壞死性傷口持續 **3–6 個月**不癒合且無腫瘤復發；risk factors：**fraction size >2 Gy**、照射骨體積、**骨總劑量 >60 Gy**、re-irradiation、particle/brachytherapy/kilovoltage energy、口腔衛生不良與拔牙。
  （Grabb Ch20 對應數字：ORN 「typically considered **greater than 60 Gy**」。）

- **⚠ Neligan Vol4 Ch08 唯一唱反調**：「It is particularly useful in **radiation-induced wounds**, frostbite, revascularized gangrenous feet, and failing flaps.」

**👉 ORN 的口試裁決建議**：「**現代立場：ORN 的確定性治療是徹底清創／節段性下顎骨切除＋血管化組織（free fibula）重建，HBO 不是治療主軸**；Grabb 9e Ch20 直言歷史研究對 ORN 未顯示明顯效益，Neligan Vol4 Ch11 引 Annane 2004 ORN96 RCT 稱 limited success。HBO 目前較被支持的是**放射後軟組織晚期毒性的恢復**（Grabb Ch20 引 2016／2023 乳癌系列研究），以及 ORN 的**預防性應用**（拔牙前後）—— 但後者屬課本外。Marx 的 30+10 protocol 在這兩本書中完全沒有出現。」
