# 六、軟組織惡性腫瘤　〔潘信誠〕🟠 手冊只有零星 DFSP／angiosarcoma

Neligan Vol 1 的 tumor 章／Grabb 的 soft tissue sarcoma 章。

> **本節實際查得的主力章節**（原任務清單未列，但這是課本裡真正談 STS 的專章）：
> **Neligan 5e Vol 4 Ch 4 "Lower extremity sarcoma reconstruction"**（Drive fileId `1uMSPhx5jyJxWiydcXKciNhuRto_q3hPq`）。
> 其餘引用：Neligan Vol 1 Ch 29（非黑色素細胞皮膚／軟組織腫瘤）、Neligan Vol 1 Ch 27（放射治療原則）、Neligan Vol 1 Ch 14（癌症處置原則）、Grabb 9e Ch 17（皮膚良惡性病灶）、Grabb 9e Ch 99（Hand Tumors）、Grabb 9e Ch 20（Radiation and Radiation Injury）。

---

### §6.1 ⭐⭐ 切片原則 🟢
**最常考、也最容易答錯的一項。**我的認知：
1. **先做影像（MRI），再切片** —— 切片會造成 artifact 干擾判讀
2. 切口走**縱向**（沿肢體長軸），絕不橫向
3. 切口必須落在**日後根除手術會一併切掉的路徑上**
4. 徹底止血、盡量不放引流；一定要放的話，出口貼近切口，日後一併切除
5. **絕不做 enucleation／shelling out** —— 會留 residual disease
要確認：課本現在主推 **core needle biopsy** 還是 incisional biopsy？

課本：

**逐條裁決（🟢 對／錯）**

| # | 你的認知 | 判定 | 課本原句／出處 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 先影像再切片 | **✅ 對** | 「**Any imaging of the tumor region must be performed before any surgical biopsy as the latter may confound the picture to a considerable extent.**」— Neligan Vol4 Ch4「Clinical assessment and staging」。Neligan Vol1 Ch29 亦寫：「biopsies should only take place **after** careful inspection, palpation, and imaging analyses」 |
| 2 | 縱向切口、絕不橫向 | **✅ 對（且課本把橫切口列為「必須重做手術」的指標）** | 「Usually, a **longitudinal incision** as short as possible for an adequate exposure directly over the tumor is made, providing the shortest reasonable access route to the neoplasm.」「**An incision parallel to the axis of the extremity is made.**」— Neligan Vol4 Ch4。<br>再手術指標：「Some indicators for a re-operation are **incisions placed horizontally to the axis of the extremity** and opening up several muscle compartments…」<br>Grabb Ch99 生檢原則清單第 2 條即「**Longitudinal skin incisions**」 |
| 3 | 切口落在日後切除路徑上 | **✅ 對** | 「**Before incising the skin, it is absolutely necessary that the surgeon already imagines any secondary, definitive tumor resections** and has possible muscle and tendon transfers and local flap options in mind. The biopsy incision should interfere as little as possible with these factors.」— Neligan Vol4 Ch4<br>「The resection must include **all previous skin incisions, all previous stitch marks, and drain sites** with a margin of **4 cm** of healthy skin around them.」— 同章 definitive resection 段<br>Neligan Vol1 Ch14：「surgical incisions for open biopsies should be planned carefully **to enable excision of the biopsy with the tumor specimen**（e.g., sarcomas and other soft tissue tumors）」<br>Grabb Ch99：「include the **biopsy track as an en bloc specimen** to prevent any potential seeding」；「The definitive plan for resection should be made **ahead of** the initial biopsy incision」；「Ideally, the surgeon performing the final resection should also be the one who performed the initial biopsy」 |
| 4 | 徹底止血、**盡量不放引流** | **⚠ 前半對、後半錯 —— 課本主張「一定要放 closed suction drain」** | 「**Meticulous hemostasis and the placement of closed suction drainage within the wound to prevent possible tumor seeding through a hematoma are paramount** before a multilayered skin closure is done.」— Neligan Vol4 Ch4（引流是**必要**，不是「盡量不放」；目的正是防血腫散播腫瘤細胞）<br>引流出口位置：「**Mattress sutures or separate drainage perforations leave stitch marks too far away from the incision** and, as they all have to be excised in case of malignancy, this would enlarge the amount of tissue to be resected.」→ 你「出口貼近切口、日後一併切除」的敘述 **✅ 完全正確**<br>Grabb Ch99 原則清單：「**Drains placed in line with incision**」「**Sutures as close to the edges as possible**」「Meticulous hemostasis」<br>另附加：**tourniquet 但不做 exsanguination**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4：「with a pressurized tourniquet (**no exsanguination!**)」）；Grabb Ch99 寫「**Gravity exsanguination**」（兩本略有出入，Grabb 允許重力排血、Neligan 主張完全不排血）<br>**VAC 禁忌**：「Both incisional and excisional biopsies are not intended to adequately remove the tumor… **temporary vacuum-assisted closure techniques should be avoided**」（切片後禁 VAC；但**wide resection 後可用 VAC**，同章明言無證據顯示影響腫瘤學安全性） |
| 5 | 絕不 enucleation／shelling out | **✅ 對（課本用不同措辭表達同一原則）** | 課本**沒有**逐字寫「never enucleate」，但寫得更嚴格：「**Any palpable pseudocapsule of the tumor belongs to the tumor itself and should be resected but not opened or seen at all, otherwise the procedure is regarded as an R1-resection.**」「The tumor should not be retracted or manipulated with sharp or pointed-tipped instruments」— Neligan Vol4 Ch4<br>Neligan Vol1 Ch29 用切緣定義：「**marginal excision** indicates margins **within the tumor-reactive layer**」（＝沿假包膜剝除），並明言「**wide excision is appropriate margin for soft tissue sarcoma**」<br>再手術強制指標亦包含：「A clinically unsuspicious and presumably benign tumor was resected with **the techniques of benign tumor surgery**, but the postoperative histopathologic workup showed an unexpected malignancy」<br>⚠ 注意反例：**schwannoma** 才是課本明白允許「**shelling out**」的（Grabb Ch99；Neligan Vol1 Ch29 亦然）——口試若被問到，要能區分良性神經鞘瘤 vs 肉瘤 |

**❖ core needle biopsy vs incisional biopsy —— 課本主推哪一個？**

**Neligan Vol4 Ch4 的立場：incisional biopsy 是 gold standard，core needle 是次要／特定情境用。** 原文：

- 「**The surgical resection of a representative part of the sarcoma is the gold standard in diagnosis of extremity tumors for any lesions that are larger than 3–5 cm and of subfascial location.**」
- Fine needle / core needle 的限制：「both methods have the disadvantage of **not representing the tumor tissue components correctly**, especially in larger tumors, which makes it very difficult for the histopathologist to find the exact diagnosis, perform the necessary number of different studies, and **determine the correct grading**.」
- 「However, **if combined with CT scan or ultrasound-guided needle placement, core biopsies may achieve a correct diagnosis in up to 90% of cases** under optimal circumstances. **Fine-needle aspirations only reach 56%–72%.**」
- 「In several institutions, **core biopsies are reserved for surgically unresectable tumors (i.e., retroperitoneal lesions)** to determine tissue type and guide an eventual neoadjuvant therapy, while **others use it as a prime diagnostic tissue sampling method**.」
- 結論句：「**In summary, fine needle biopsies do not have a place in sarcoma diagnosis in the lower extremities**, and **both core biopsy and open surgical biopsy are very operator dependent**: if poorly performed, the risk of getting an inadequate diagnostic sample or tumor seeding is high, respectively.」
- 骨性病灶：「**Bone-forming lesions are very difficult to adequately sample percutaneously, and open biopsies are preferable.**」

**❖ 兩本不一致處**：Neligan Vol4 Ch4 的治療演算法（Algorithm 4.1）第一格卻寫「Histologic diagnosis of suspicious tumor (**core needle or incisional biopsy**)」——並列，不分主次。Grabb Ch99 亦並列：「Type of biopsy (**core needle, incisional, excisional**)」。→ **口試安全講法**：「影像先行；**core needle（影像導引）與 incisional biopsy 皆為現行標準**，但課本明言 **incisional biopsy 對肢體 >3–5 cm、subfascial 病灶仍是 gold standard**（分級 grading 需要足量組織），**FNA 在肉瘤診斷沒有地位**。」

**❖ 課本另外要求、你清單漏掉的**（口試加分點，Neligan Vol4 Ch4）：
1. **組織量**：incisional biopsy 應取「**at least 2 × 1 × 1 cm**」的組織塊，且要「**from all areas of the tumor including the capsule**」——「It is a common misconception to want to gain tissue only from the central portion, as this area often contains considerable amounts of **necrotic tissue**」
2. **no-touch**：檢體「should be manipulated as little as possible and **should not come into contact with the wound edges** of the surgical access route」；「**Any instrument used to hold or manipulate the biopsy specimen must not be used for wound closure or retraction**」；取下腫瘤塊後「**both instruments and gloves are changed**」
3. **關創**：single interrupted 或 **intracutaneous sutures**；**禁 mattress suture**（stitch mark 離切口太遠）
4. **術後**：無菌環形加壓敷料＋患肢固定（splint／external fixator 數日）＋抬高＋臥床——目的是**防血腫／seroma → 防腫瘤散播**
5. **external fixator pin**：「**A fixator pin must never be set into an area for later resection**, or the pin tract has to be included in the resection specimen」
6. **Excisional biopsy 的適應症**：僅限「**less than 3–5 cm in diameter and with epifascial location**」

**❖ 必須重做手術（re-operation mandatory）的三個情境**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4，這是很好的口試題）：
1. 第一次手術只取得**不足以做確定病理**的組織
2. 前次切除**不符現行腫瘤學指引**——指標：**橫向切口**、打穿數個 muscle compartment、「切除 MPNST」後神經功能卻完好、「radical excision of extensor/flexor muscles」後下肢運動功能卻正常
3. 臨床判為良性、以**良性腫瘤手術方式**切除，術後病理卻是惡性

---

### §6.2 影像與分期 🟡
我的認知：MRI with contrast 是 gold standard；胸部 CT 找肺轉移。
AJCC 8th 的 T 分期改成 T1 ≤5 cm、T2 >5–10、T3 >10–15、T4 >15 cm。
要確認：課本用第幾版？grade 用 FNCLCC（分化 ＋ mitosis ＋ necrosis）嗎？

課本：

**（一）影像 —— 你的認知 ✅ 正確**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4）

- **MRI（gadolinium 顯影）是診斷主軸**：「**Gadolinium contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is currently the diagnostic mainstay** to define exact tumor location, its relation to neighboring neurovascular structures and muscular compartments, to determine its homogeneity, integrity, and vascularization, and its presumed main tissue component. **MRI is specifically useful for detecting skip lesions.** It allows 3D planning of the resection…」
- **CT 找轉移**：「**Thoracic and abdominal CT scans are the diagnostics of choice for staging of high-grade sarcomas of the extremities and detect intrapulmonary and abdominal metastases.**」（注意：課本是**胸＋腹 CT**，不只胸部）
- **低分級用胸部 X 光即可**：「**A plain chest radiograph is still considered the standard for clinical staging in low-grade extremity lesions.**」（這句是考點）
- **PET-CT**：「In recurrent disease, PET-CT can augment the information about suspicious lesions in selected cases, **though not accepted as a standard instrument for the preoperative workup**」
- **CT angiography**：評估血管狀態、腫瘤血管侵犯、以及**顯微吻合的 recipient vessel 可行性**
- **Plain X-ray**：「**a plain radiograph remains the diagnostic method of choice for primary bone sarcomas**」
- **99m-Tc bone scan**：「essential to bone tumor staging and screening for multicentric disease or metastases」
- **Ultrasound**：便宜、快、無痛的輔助，尤其高血管性腫瘤；operator dependent
- **實驗室**：「A special laboratory workup for soft-tissue sarcomas **does not exist**」；但**骨肉瘤** ALP 與 **LDH > 400 U/L** 是預後不良的獨立預測因子
- **追蹤**：「Contrast-enhanced MRI of the primary tumor site **every 3 months**」，且 MRI 亦是評估淋巴擴散的首選

**（二）AJCC 版本 —— 課本用 8th edition ✅，你的四段分界完全正確 ✅**

Neligan Vol4 Ch4 原文：
> 「Finally, tumor staging is done according to the current staging systems for soft-tissue sarcomas. The **American Joint Committee on Cancer (AJCC) eighth edition** system is designed for extremity sarcomas, including most but not all histologic subtypes. The **major changes in the eighth edition** … are the following four points:
> (i) Tumors are described separately according to the primary sites. For **bone sarcoma, three tumor locations**: (a) appendicular skeleton, trunk, skull and facial bones; (b) spine; (c) pelvis. **Four tumor locations for soft-tissue sarcoma**: (1) trunk and extremity; (2) retroperitoneum; (3) head and neck; (4) visceral sites.
> (ii) **Histologic grading system in bone sarcoma is changed to three-grade classifications.**
> (iii) For soft-tissue sarcoma, **any TN1M0 tumor in the trunk and extremity is classified as stage IV**, whereas for the retroperitoneal tumor, any TN1M0 remains as **stage IIIB**.
> (iv) For soft-tissue sarcomas in the trunk, extremity, and retroperitoneum, tumor size was classified into **four categories: (a) ≤5 cm; (b) >5 cm and ≤10 cm; (c) >10 cm and ≤15 cm; (d) >15 cm**. In addition, the **notation about the depth of the tumor (superficial or deep from the superficial fascia) has been eliminated.**」

**⭐ 三個你清單上沒有、但課本特別點出的高頻考點：**
1. **深度（superficial/deep）在 8th edition 已被刪除**——這是 7th→8th 最愛考的改變
2. **肢體／軀幹 STS 只要 N1（淋巴結陽性）就直接跳 stage IV**；腹膜後則仍是 IIIB
3. **「DFSP and angiosarcoma, among others, are exempt from staging with the AJCC.」**（DFSP 與 angiosarcoma **不適用** AJCC 分期）

**（三）骨肉瘤另用 MSTS（Enneking）系統**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4, Table 4.2）：

| Stage | Characteristic |
|---|---|
| IA | Low-grade, **intra**compartmental |
| IB | Low-grade, **extra**compartmental |
| IIA | High-grade, intracompartmental |
| IIB | High-grade, extracompartmental |
| IIIA | Low- or high-grade, intracompartmental **with metastases** |
| IIIB | Low- or high-grade, extracompartmental **with metastases** |

「This classification was established in **1980 by Enneking**… it is still the primary staging system taught in many training programs despite major evidence-based updates to the AJCC system」

**（四）FNCLCC —— ⚠ 課本查無明確出處**

> **⚠ 課本查無明確出處**（已查：Neligan Vol4 Ch4「Lower extremity sarcoma reconstruction」全章、Neligan Vol1 Ch14「Principles of cancer management」、Neligan Vol1 Ch29、Grabb 9e Ch17／Ch99；關鍵字：`FNCLCC`、`Trojani`、`mitotic`、`mitosis`、`necrosis`、`differentiation`、`grading`、`three-grade`、`Broders`）

**四本章節都沒有出現 "FNCLCC" 這個字，也沒有列出「分化＋mitosis＋necrosis」三參數表。** 課本只寫到：

- Neligan Vol1 Ch14：「**sarcoma behavior correlates with the number of mitoses identified per high-power field or histologic subtype**」（僅提 mitosis 與 subtype，未提 necrosis／differentiation 三項制）
- 但 **參考文獻明確指向 FNCLCC 系統的兩篇原始論文**，等於課本間接採用：
  - Neligan Vol4 Ch4 ref 5：**Coindre** 等,「…histopathologic grading system for adult soft tissue sarcoma. *Cancer*. 1986;58:306–309」
  - Neligan Vol4 Ch4 ref 14：**Trojani M, Contesso G, Coindre JM**, et al. 「Soft-tissue sarcomas of adults; study of pathological prognostic variables and **definition of a histopathological grading system**. *Int J Cancer*. 1984;33:37–42」（＝FNCLCC 的原始文獻）
  - Neligan Vol1 Ch14 ref 10：**Coindre JM. Grading of soft tissue sarcomas: review and update.**
- Neligan Vol4 Ch4 全章只用 **G1／G2／G3** 與 **low-grade / intermediate / high-grade** 二分或三分敘述（如 case study 寫「synovial-cell sarcoma (**G3**)」「liposarcoma **G2**」「MFH **G2**」），並定義：「usually **well-differentiated tumors show low-grade characteristics and vice versa**」

→ **口試講法**：「課本本身未逐條列出 FNCLCC 三參數，但引用的正是 Trojani／Coindre 原始文獻；**分級採三級制（G1–G3）**，AJCC 8th 亦將骨肉瘤改為三級制。你答『**FNCLCC = 分化 differentiation + mitotic count + tumor necrosis，總分 2–3 為 G1、4–5 為 G2、6–8 為 G3**』是通行標準，但要知道**這不是這兩本課本白紙黑字寫的**。」

---

### §6.3 切緣 🟡
我的認知：現行是「**達到 R0 陰性切緣**」而不是固定公分數；規劃時常取 1–2 cm，或以一層未受侵犯的 **barrier fascia** 為界。
要確認：課本給的是公分數還是原則？

課本：

**答案：兩本都給了 —— 大原則是 R0，但「公分數」也白紙黑字寫出來了，而且各章數字不同（🟡 這幾組數字要抄）。**

**（一）大原則 = R0**（你的認知 ✅ 對）

Neligan Vol4 Ch4：
- 「Above all, the prime goal should be an **R0-resection with tumor-free and adequately wide margins**, which provides the best chance of complete surgical cure of the disease.」
- 「If complete tumor resection (**R0**) is possible with acceptable functional outcome, **no other treatment modality provides better cure for sarcomas than adequate surgery**.」
- **Table 4.3　WHO classification of tumor resection margins**：
  - **R0** = Resection with **microscopically tumor-free** specimen margins
  - **R1** = Resection with **microscopically tumor-positive** specimen margins
  - **R2** = Resection with **macroscopically remaining** tumor
- 若無法治癒：「resection of as much tumor mass as possible (**tumor debulking, R1/R2**) is paramount and usually followed by adjuvant radio- and (less frequently) chemotherapy」

Neligan Vol1 Ch14 亦定義同一套：「Tumor excision with **no evidence of tumor cells (either gross or microscopic) at any of the resection margins**… is commonly referred to as an **R0 resection**. In contrast, **R1 and R2** resections are defined as **positive microscopic or macroscopic margins**, respectively.」

**（二）課本給的實際公分數（🟡 抄）**

| 出處 | 數字 |
|---|---|
| **Neligan Vol4 Ch4**（下肢肉瘤章，主力） | 廣泛切除留「**a cuff of macroscopically unaffected tissue of 2–5 cm**」；「**many centers have recommended a margin of 2 cm from the deep surface of the tumor and 4–5 cm laterally**」 |
| **Neligan Vol4 Ch4** Case 4.3（小腿 STS） | 「wide excision to leave a cuff of tissue surrounding the tumor of **at least 2 cm to the depth and at least 4 cm to the sides**」 |
| **Neligan Vol4 Ch4** 前次切口／針孔／引流口 | 一併切除，四周留「**a margin of 4 cm of healthy skin**」（潰瘍性腫瘤同此） |
| **Neligan Vol1 Ch29 Box 29.4**（＝Grabb 9e Ch17 Table 17.1 引用同一表） | **Sarcoma 水平切緣：low risk 10 mm／high risk 20 mm**（1 cm／2 cm） |
| **Neligan Vol1 Ch29** 三種切除定義 | **curative wide** ＝ tumor-reactive layer 外 **≥5 cm**；**wide** ＝ tumor-reactive layer 外 **1–2 cm**；**marginal** ＝ **within** the tumor-reactive layer。並結論：「**It is now considered that wide excision is appropriate margin for soft tissue sarcoma.**」 |
| **Grabb 9e Ch99**（Hand Tumors） | 「Although not universally agreed, **recommended margins for soft tissue sarcomas are 1 cm, or any resection abutting an adjacent anatomical layer such as fascia, tendon, bone, or muscle**」 |

**⚠ 兩本／各章不一致，兩個都寫下來**：
- **Neligan Vol4 Ch4（下肢）**：深部 **2 cm**、側向 **4–5 cm**
- **Neligan Vol1 Ch29／Grabb Ch17 表**：low risk **1 cm**、high risk **2 cm**
- **Grabb Ch99（手部）**：**1 cm，或以一層解剖屏障為界**；且手部因鄰近重要結構「**might require narrower margins**」

**（三）barrier fascia／anatomic barrier —— ✅ 課本明確有寫，而且這是垂直切緣的核心邏輯**

Neligan Vol1 Ch29 Box 29.4（Grabb 9e Ch17 Table 17.1 逐字轉載）**垂直切緣**：

> **Vertical margin**
> - **With fat layer** — Tumors that are limited to the dermis
> - **With deep fascia** — Tumors that extend into the fat layer
> - **With skeletal muscle** — Tumors that extend into the deep fascia
> - **With the membranes of deeper structures such as cartilage or bone** — Tumors that extend into skeletal muscle
> - **With deeper structures like cartilage or bone** — Tumors that extend into membranes of deeper structures
>
> **Others**：「The margins of wide tumor excisions that occur in anatomically complicated and special regions (eg, eyelid, vulva, penis, finger/toe tip, and ear) will vary on a case-by-case basis. This is also true for soft-tissue tumors. **In general, at least one layer of barrier structure should be excised.** For example, for tumors that are limited to the fat layer, **the deep fascia of skeletal muscle is considered as the barrier structure and should be removed with the tumor upon wide excision.**」

→ **你的「以一層未受侵犯的 barrier fascia 為界」✅ 完全正確，且是課本原話（"at least one layer of barrier structure should be excised"）。**

**（四）現代化觀念（Neligan Vol4 Ch4 明確寫出，考官愛聽）**

> 「Still there are **no conclusive studies about the exact margins** in sarcoma surgery… **More recent studies could not establish a strong association between radical resections and improved local control or survival. Previous radical concepts in STS surgery have been gradually replaced by more moderate approaches with function- and limb-sparing resections combined with radiotherapy.** Here, the margin status appears to be of prognostic significance. However, several large retrospective analyses have presented inconsistent results, **questioning the independent prognostic impact of surgical margins**.」

Neligan Vol1 Ch14 同調：「**margins greater than 2 cm have shown no benefit**（in melanoma）」；並提醒「Although margins are frequently reported as 'negative', **recurrences often occur**」→ 這就是為何要加放療。

**（五）Pseudocapsule 是腫瘤的一部分（§6.1 已引，切緣段再強調一次）**
「**Any palpable pseudocapsule of the tumor belongs to the tumor itself and should be resected but not opened or seen at all, otherwise the procedure is regarded as an R1-resection.**」——課本稱之為 **no-touch／no-see technique**。

**（六）標本處理（口試常被追問）**
「It is extremely important to **clearly mark the decisive anatomic landmarks and the topographic orientation of the tumor in its previous bed** to allow unequivocal and exact determination of margins by the pathologist. **Optimally, the examining histopathologist is present at this stage of the procedure.**」
Grabb Ch99：「the surgeon can use **surgical clips to mark the margins of resection**」；「It is important for the surgeon to **review the specimen with the pathologist**」

**（七）血管／神經／骨受侵的處理原則**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4，配合切緣一起答）
- **血管**：未被直接侵犯／包覆者 →「**longitudinal opening of the vascular adventitia opposite the tumor and subsequent microsurgical stripping of the adventitia** under loupe magnification and **en bloc with the tumor**」；完全被包覆 → en bloc 切除＋vein patch／vein graft／人工血管
- **神經**：同樣策略；「If **only a few fascicles** of an important main nerve trunk are adherent to the tumor, it is considered **acceptable to resect these** and leave the unaffected nerve bundles intact」；「If a **major nerve is encased completely, it must be resected** and should be reconstructed primarily or secondarily」
- **⭐「Not being able to preserve the main nerves and thereby sensitivity is NOT an indication for amputation by itself!」**（課本驚嘆號原文，非常適合口試引用）
- **骨**：periosteal stripping／decortication／partial bone resection 皆可；但要警覺**術後放療＋骨質疏鬆 → 自發性骨折**風險

---

### §6.4 ⭐ 放療時機 🔴
我的認知（NCIC SR2 trial）：
- **術前**：劑量低（50 Gy）、照野小，但**傷口併發症 35%**
- **術後**：66 Gy、照野大、長期纖維化多，傷口併發症 **17%**
**這組數字要抄。**

課本：

**✅ 你的數字幾乎全對，只有一處要修正：術後劑量課本寫 60–66 Gy（不是單一 66 Gy）。**

**❖ 出處：Neligan 5e Vol 1 Ch 27「Principles of radiation therapy」，"Sarcoma → RT indications, dose, and techniques" 節。以下🔴 整段照抄：**

> **RT indications, dose, and techniques**
>
> For STS, RT is indicated for tumors **>5 cm, intermediate or high grade regardless of margin status**, or **low-grade STS with positive margins**, local recurrence s/p surgery alone, or a tumor location-sensitive and challenging location for surgical salvage.
>
> The **Canadian NCIC-SR-2 clinical trial** demonstrated the **same local control** with **preoperative (50 Gy in 25 fractions)** versus **postoperative radiotherapy (60–66 Gy in 30–33 fractions)**, with **decreased permanent late grade 2 or greater fibrosis, joint stiffness, and edema**. The lower preoperative dose and smaller field size was thought to result in these reduced late toxicities, since **a higher dose is required in the postoperative setting due to decreased oxygenation and vascularity of the tumor bed** along with the need to **cover the whole operative "footprint"** which can result in larger field sizes (fusing a postoperative diagnostic MRI to identify the surgical bed along with the operative report is essential). **The tradeoff was that preoperative RT resulted in a higher rate of major wound healing complications than postoperative RT (35% vs. 17%).** **Wound complications varied dramatically by anatomic site with nearly no major wound complications in the upper extremity, but a more pronounced rate of complications in the lower extremity with preoperative radiation.** Despite this increased risk, **given wound healing complications are generally considered reversible, many institutions favor a preoperative approach.** Algorithm 27.2 summarizes the multidisciplinary clinical decision-making for STS in regard to pre- versus postoperative radiotherapy.
>
> It is important to realize that **the NCIC-SR-2 trial was conducted before IMRT was in common use**. Subsequent data supports that **more conformal radiotherapy techniques such as IMRT and reduced treatment field size can result in improved local control**, along with **decreased wound healing complications particularly with collaboration with the surgical team**, and late complications, which can lead to more cost-effective care.
>
> **Proton therapy is considered for inguinal or pelvic sarcomas**, particularly in young or adolescent patients to minimize the radiotherapy dose to the pelvic organs to maximize the opportunity for fertility sparing.
>
> Optimal immobilization is important in the RT planning process. It is also necessary to delineate bones, joints, perineum, gonadal and pelvic structures so that dose to these sites can be minimized. **It is also necessary to spare a longitudinal strip of skin and subcutaneous tissue so that lymphatic drainage remains intact to reduce the risk of chronic lymphedema.**

**❖ Algorithm 27.2（Neligan Vol1 Ch27）🔴 對照表照抄：**

| | **Preop RT considerations** | **Postop RT considerations** |
|---|---|---|
| 劑量 | **Lower dose (50 Gy)** | **Higher dose (60–66 Gy)** |
| 療程長度 | Shorter treatment duration: **5–5.5 weeks** | Longer treatment duration: **6–6.5 weeks** |
| 照野 | **Smaller field size** | **Larger field size** |
| 纖維化 | **Reduced fibrosis** | **Increased fibrosis** |
| 水腫 | **Reduced edema** | **Increased edema** |
| 傷口 | **Increased wound healing complications** | **Decreased wound complications** |
| 局部控制 | \<— **Equivalent local control** —> | |

演算法左右分流：**>5 cm、intermediate-high grade**（likely will require RT）→ 多專科評估是否 neoadjuvant chemo；另一支則「review to determine if high-risk features are present to necessitate postoperative radiation」（may not require RT）。並明列一格：「**Discussion with oncologic surgical team regarding clinical experience with preop vs. post RT**」。

**❖ SR-2 原始文獻（Neligan Vol1 Ch27 ref 64, 72, 73）**
- **O'Sullivan B, Davis AM, Turcotte R, et al. Preoperative versus postoperative radiotherapy in soft-tissue sarcoma of the limbs: a randomised trial. *Lancet*. 2002;359(9325):2235–2241.**
- Davis AM, O'Sullivan B, Turcotte R, et al. **Late radiation morbidity** following randomization to preoperative versus postoperative radiotherapy in extremity soft tissue sarcoma. *Radiother Oncol*. 2005;75(1):48–53.
- Davis AM, O'Sullivan B, Bell RS, et al. Function and health status outcomes… *J Clin Oncol*. 2002;20(22):4472–4477.

**❖ Neligan Vol1 Ch27 其他相關數字（🟡 順帶抄）**
- 微觀病灶（如 STS 周圍 peritumoral edema）用 **45–50 cGy〔原文如此，應為 45–50 Gy〕**；切緣病理陽性時需更高劑量
- **骨折預防**：「the **maximum dose to the bone should be 59 Gy**, and that the **volume of bone receiving >40 Gy be limited to 64%**」，並避免**環周照射到 50 Gy**
- **IORT（術中放療）**：單次電子束，用於 3–10 cm 高復發風險區（如腹膜後肉瘤）；下肢適應症在**腹股溝與足部**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4）
- Neutron RT：對 **radioresistant tumors such as sarcomas** 生物效應高（LET 高 20–100 倍），但晚期毒性重

**❖ Neligan Vol4 Ch4（外科端）對放療時機的敘述（補充、與 Ch27 一致）**
> 「**Neoadjuvant radiation** in large sarcomas uses external beam irradiation that helps in **tumor shrinkage and thickening of the tumor capsule**, which **facilitates adequate resection and the achievement of negative margins** during wide resection and **reduces potential surgical tumor seeding**. The **disadvantages of preoperative irradiation are a higher rate of wound healing complications** compared to postoperative radiotherapy and **the creation of necrotic tumor material for the pathologist**. **However, preoperative radiotherapy did not increase the risk of acute wound or microvascular complications when combined with free flap reconstruction, and was associated with fewer late recipient-site complications than adjuvant irradiation.**」
>
> 「Patients after sarcoma resection with adequate margins and R0 situation **need postoperative adjuvant radiation therapy under most circumstances, especially in tumors that are classified as high-grade (G2/G3). Only superficial, low-grade lesions with tumor-free wide margins may be treated with wide resection only.** The radiation oncologists start with the therapy… **as soon as the wound is healed.**」
>
> **ESMO 建議**：「favors adjuvant radiotherapy when **tumor size is above 5 cm or deeply located or high grade** and when **R1 resection** occurs」
>
> **術後放療方式**：「Postoperative irradiation is done with **brachytherapy and electron beam therapy**… **Brachytherapy is especially useful after resection of local recurrences in a previously irradiated field.**」
>
> **新藥**：**NBTXR3**（radioenhancer，phase 1–2）與放療併用可提升反應率

**❖ Grabb 9e Ch 20「Radiation and Radiation Injury」**：有 "SARCOMA RECONSTRUCTION" 一節，但**只談重建策略、不給劑量與併發症率**：
> 「In some circumstances, radiation is administered in a **preoperative** setting, whereas other sarcoma subtypes are best treated with **adjuvant** radiotherapy. As most soft tissue sarcomas occur in the **lower extremity**, the majority of defects can be closed with **local flaps, which are clearly in the radiation field and have higher risks for impaired wound healing, infection**… As such for the majority of patients who undergo treatment of sarcoma, **reconstruction often requires more complex reconstruction using a microvascular free flap or perhaps a local regional flap that is beyond the zone of injury from the radiation**.」
> 另提：肉瘤（尤其下肢）病人也是**淋巴水腫**高危險群；因多數接受 neoadjuvant RT，**切除當下做 preventative lymphatic reconstruction（immediate lymphatic reconstruction）** 的淋巴管未受照射，修復效果與耐久度較佳。
> ⚠ **Grabb Ch20 未提 NCIC SR-2、未給 Gy 數與 35%/17%**（已查關鍵字：`SR-2`、`NCIC`、`Gy`、`preoperative radiation`、`wound complication`）。

---

### §6.5 淋巴結 🟢
我的認知：STS 極少走淋巴轉移，**例外**要背：rhabdomyosarcoma、epithelioid sarcoma、clear cell sarcoma、angiosarcoma、synovial sarcoma。

課本：

**🟢 前半 ✅ 對；後半 ⚠ 課本的例外清單只有三個，比你背的少兩個。**

**（一）STS 極少淋巴轉移 —— ✅ 對，且課本給了數字**

Neligan Vol4 Ch4：
- 「**Hematogenic spread is most common in soft-tissue and bone sarcomas.** For lower extremity tumors, **the primary site for metastasis is the lung.** **Lymphatic metastases are present in less than 5% of all soft-tissue sarcomas** (rhabdomyosarcoma, angiosarcoma, and epithelioid-like sarcoma).」
- 「The pertaining lymph node stations should be examined as well, **even though lymphatic spread is uncommon in the majority of all sarcoma types**.」

Neligan Vol1 Ch14 對比：「**carcinomas drain through regional nodal basins, in contrast to sarcomas that spread hematogenously**」

Neligan Vol1 Ch27：「it is **uncommon for most STS subtypes to spread to regional nodal basins**」

**（二）例外清單 —— ⚠ 課本只列三個**

Neligan Vol4 Ch4「Lymph node dissection」節原文：
> 「**Less than 5% of all sarcomas spread in a lymphatic way.** Therefore there is **no indication for a standardized simultaneous dissection** of the relevant lymph node areas (i.e., popliteal fossa/groin) **if the region is not clearly involved clinically or radiographically**. A **lymph node dissection should always be performed, however, in sarcomatous tumor entities that are known for their lymphatic spread like the rhabdomyosarcoma, angiosarcoma, and epithelioid-like sarcoma subtypes.** **If regional lymph node metastases are present, a radical lymphadenectomy significantly improves median survival.**」

| 你背的 5 個 | 課本（Neligan Vol4 Ch4）有無 |
|---|---|
| **Rhabdomyosarcoma** | ✅ 有 |
| **Angiosarcoma** | ✅ 有 |
| **Epithelioid sarcoma** | ✅ 有（課本寫 "**epithelioid-like sarcoma**"） |
| **Clear cell sarcoma** | ❌ **課本未列** |
| **Synovial sarcoma** | ❌ **課本未列** |

> **⚠ 課本查無明確出處**（clear cell sarcoma 與 synovial sarcoma 作為淋巴轉移例外）
> 已查：Neligan Vol4 Ch4「Lower extremity sarcoma reconstruction」全章、Neligan Vol1 Ch29、Neligan Vol1 Ch14、Neligan Vol1 Ch27；關鍵字：`lymph`、`lymph node`、`clear cell`、`synovial`、`lymphatic spread`、`lymphadenectomy`。
> Neligan Vol1 Ch29 僅在「Other malignant mesenchymal-origin tumors」一句帶過 **epithelioid sarcoma、synovial sarcoma、extraskeletal Ewing's sarcoma、histiocytic sarcoma、Langerhans cell sarcoma**，但**是在治療（induction chemo → wide excision → maintenance chemo）脈絡，不是淋巴轉移脈絡**。
> **課本引用的原始文獻**是 Fong Y, Coit DG, Woodruff JM, Brennan MF.「Lymph node metastasis from soft tissue sarcoma in adults. Analysis of data from a prospective database of 1772 sarcoma patients. *Ann Surg*. 1993;217:72–77」與 Daigeler A, et al.「Lymph node metastases in soft tissue sarcomas: a single center analysis of 1,597 patients. *Langenbecks Arch Surg*. 2009;394:321–329」——臨床上常背的 **CASSSE**（Clear cell, Angiosarcoma, Synovial, Squamous-differentiated, Epithelioid, Rhabdomyosarcoma）**不是這兩本課本的內容**。

→ **口試安全講法**：先給課本版本「**課本列出三個：rhabdomyosarcoma、angiosarcoma、epithelioid-like sarcoma，佔比 <5%**」，再補「文獻上另常列 **clear cell sarcoma 與 synovial sarcoma**」。這樣答既不失分也顯示廣度。

**（三）處理原則（要一起答）**
1. **不做預防性（routine/prophylactic）淋巴廓清**——「no indication for a standardized simultaneous dissection」
2. 只在 **臨床或影像上明確侵犯**、或屬上述三種易淋巴轉移組織型時才廓清
3. 若確有 regional LN metastasis → **radical lymphadenectomy 可顯著改善 median survival**
4. **MRI 是評估淋巴擴散的首選影像**（Neligan Vol4 Ch4 追蹤節）
5. **淋巴水腫預防**（Neligan Vol1 Ch27 + Grabb Ch20）：放療計畫要「**spare a longitudinal strip of skin and subcutaneous tissue** so that lymphatic drainage remains intact」；需廓清時可考慮 **LYMPHA procedure／immediate lymphatic reconstruction**；廣泛切除時「**the previous incision… creates an elliptical defect along the longitudinal axis of the leg. The advantages of this orientation are the preservation of the remaining subdermal lymph vessels**」

**⚠ 注意 §6.2 的交叉考點**：AJCC 8th 中，**肢體／軀幹 STS 只要 N1 就直接是 stage IV**（腹膜後則為 IIIB）——「淋巴結陽性 = 預後極差」正是這個分期改動的意義。

---

### §6.6 DFSP 🟡
我的認知：CD34 陽性、COL1A1-PDGFB 融合基因、切緣 2–4 cm 或 Mohs、無法切除者用 imatinib。

課本：

**✅ 四項全對，但兩本書的切緣數字不同，兩個都要記。**

**❖ Grabb 9e Ch 17「Benign and Malignant Dermatologic Conditions」— DERMATOFIBROSARCOMA PROTUBERANS（🔴 整段照抄，最貼近你的認知）**

> Dermatofibrosarcoma protuberans (DFSP) is a **rare, dermal soft-tissue sarcoma** that typically affects **middle-aged adults in the third to fifth decade** of life. DFSP originates from **dermal fibroblasts** and presents as a **slow-growing, firm, and fixed plaque or nodule** that may be flesh-colored, pink, or brown. Clinically, a diagnosis can be made by skin biopsy. **Histopathologically, it is classically composed of uniform spindle cell fascicles with a storiform, infiltrative growth pattern with strong CD34 immunoreactivity.** DFSP is known for its **propensity to recur after incomplete excision because of its ability to infiltrate surrounding tissue.** Surgical excision is the standard for treatment with **wide local excision or Mohs**. Many studies have cited **lower recurrence rates for Mohs micrographic excision**. However, **Mohs has a higher medical cost, requires delayed closure, and may be time-consuming.** **There is no agreement on the standard optimal width of DFSP. However, a 2- to 4-cm lateral margin is generally acceptable, with the excision of investing fascia.** Should margins be positive and no further resection is possible, **radiotherapy can be considered as DFSP is responsive to radiation.** **Imatinib mesylate can be used both as a neoadjuvant and an adjuvant targeted therapy in treatment for unresectable disease.**

**❖ Neligan 5e Vol 1 Ch 29 — Dermatofibrosarcoma protuberans（🔴 整段照抄）**

> DFSP is also called **giant cell fibroblastoma**. **Over 90% of DFSP tumors have the chromosomal translocation t(17;22) that fuses the collagen gene COL1A1 with the platelet-derived growth factor gene.** Typically, DFSP occurs as multiple or solitary tumors that have a **red color, hemispherical elevation, and papillary vessels on the surface**. They have a **keloidal appearance, which has sometimes led to misdiagnosis and treatment with corticosteroids.** **They metastasize rarely, but distant metastasis to the lung can result from local recurrence.** **Radical resection should include a normal skin margin of 5 cm. Mohs micrographic surgery with continuous histological margin control is needed to reduce local recurrence rates.** **Adjuvant chemotherapy and radiation therapy may be useful.**

**❖ 逐項對照**

| 你的認知 | 判定 | 出處 |
|---|---|---|
| **CD34 陽性** | ✅ 對 | **Grabb Ch17**：「uniform spindle cell fascicles with a **storiform, infiltrative** growth pattern with **strong CD34 immunoreactivity**」。⚠ **Neligan Ch29 未提 CD34** |
| **COL1A1-PDGFB 融合基因** | ✅ 對 | **Neligan Vol1 Ch29**：「Over **90%** of DFSP tumors have the chromosomal translocation **t(17;22)** that fuses the collagen gene **COL1A1** with the **platelet-derived growth factor** gene」。⚠ **Grabb Ch17 未提此融合基因** |
| **切緣 2–4 cm** | ✅ 對（Grabb） **⚠ 但 Neligan 寫 5 cm** | **Grabb Ch17**：「**There is no agreement on the standard optimal width**… a **2- to 4-cm lateral margin** is generally acceptable, **with the excision of investing fascia**」<br>**Neligan Vol1 Ch29**：「Radical resection should include a normal skin margin of **5 cm**」<br>**→ 兩本不一致，口試兩個都講，並強調「無共識、以 R0／Mohs 連續切緣控制為原則」** |
| **Mohs** | ✅ 對 | **Grabb Ch17**：「**Many studies have cited lower recurrence rates for Mohs**… 但 Mohs 成本高、需延遲關創、耗時」；**Grabb Ch17 Table 17.2「Indication for Mohs excision in primary lesions」明列 DFSP**（與 perineural invasion、microcystic adnexal carcinoma 並列）<br>**Neligan Vol1 Ch29**：「**Mohs micrographic surgery with continuous histological margin control is needed to reduce local recurrence rates**」<br>Neligan Vol1 Ch29 另一處：Mohs「can help to obtain complete margin control during the removal of a **skin cancer and soft-tissue sarcoma**」 |
| **無法切除者用 imatinib** | ✅ 對，且課本比你寫得更寬 | **Grabb Ch17**：「Imatinib mesylate can be used **both as a neoadjuvant and an adjuvant** targeted therapy in treatment for **unresectable disease**」（＝新輔助也可以，不只姑息）<br>**Neligan Vol1 Ch14** 把 imatinib 歸為 protein kinase antagonist／targeted therapy 的原型範例 |

**❖ 你清單漏掉、但課本有寫的 DFSP 加分點**
1. **別名 giant cell fibroblastoma**（Neligan Ch29）
2. **外觀像 keloid**，常被誤診而打類固醇（Neligan Ch29）——非常好的口試 pitfall
3. **極少轉移；但局部復發可導致肺轉移**（Neligan Ch29）
4. **DFSP 不適用 AJCC 分期**：「**Dermatofibrosarcoma protuberans (DFSP) and angiosarcoma, among others, are exempt from staging with the AJCC**」（Neligan Vol4 Ch4）
5. **切緣陽性且無法再切 → 放療**（DFSP 對放射線敏感；Grabb Ch17）
6. **切除範圍要含 investing fascia**（Grabb Ch17）——呼應 §6.3 的 barrier structure 概念
7. **放療後遺症圖例**：Neligan Vol1 Ch27 有一張 DFSP 肩部切除後放療的照片，示範「characteristic **telangiectasia, hypopigmentation, subcutaneous fibrosis**, and delayed healing of a minor skin abrasion」

**❖ Neligan Vol1 Ch29 其他惡性間質腫瘤（順帶背，同一節）**
- **Pleomorphic undifferentiated sarcoma (PUS)**＝舊稱 MFH，四型：storiform-pleomorphic／myxoid／giant cell／inflammatory；**atypical fibroxanthoma 視為其表淺型**；現多認為 storiform-pleomorphic 型應重新分類為 liposarcoma／leiomyosarcoma／rhabdomyosarcoma
- **Liposarcoma**：診斷靠找到 **lipoblast**（豐富、透明、多空泡細胞質＋偏心深染受壓細胞核）；「A **rapidly growing lipoma** should be examined carefully to eliminate the possibility that it is actually a **liposarcoma**」
- **Angiosarcoma**：內皮細胞惡性腫瘤；「Given the location of angiosarcomas, **metastasis to distant sites occurs often**」；手術＋放療＋化療（paclitaxel／docetaxel、MAID）＋IL-2 免疫治療，**預後差**
- **Kaposi's sarcoma**：**KSHV（HHV-8）**；放療／冷凍／化療有用，**手術非首選**，須合併 HAART
- **Chondrosarcoma**：化療與傳統放療效果差，**proton beam** 有前景；完整手術切除最有效

# 七、Klippel–Trénaunay　〔彭成康〕🟡 手冊只到 ISSVA 分類層級

Neligan Vol 1／Vol 3 的 vascular anomalies 章。

> **查書範圍聲明**
> 已完整讀完並全文檢索：
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕Vascular Anomalies（Arin K. Greene）
> - 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕Vascular anomalies（**唯一主力章，KTS 專節在此**）
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch21〕Lasers in Plastic Surgery（port-wine stain 雷射）
> - 〔Grabb 9e Ch100〕Vascular Disorders of the Upper Extremity（**全章無 Klippel／Trénaunay／Parkes Weber／marginal vein 字樣**，只有泛論式 vascular anomalies 段落）
> - 已檢索 Neligan Vol1 與 Vol4 全部章節清單：**沒有第二個 vascular anomalies／vascular malformation 章**（Vol1 為總論與基礎、Vol4 為下肢軀幹燒傷淋巴水腫），故 KTS 內容僅 Vol3 Ch31 一處。
> - 檢索關鍵字：Klippel／Trenaunay／Trénaunay／Parkes Weber／marginal vein／Servelle／PIK3CA／RASA1／EPHB4／sirolimus／alpelisib／epiphysiodesis／intravascular coagulopathy／LIC／CLOVES／D-dimer／duplex／MRV／venography。

### §7.1 三徵 🟢
我的認知：① capillary malformation（port-wine stain）② 靜脈畸形／靜脈曲張，含 **lateral marginal vein of Servelle** ③ 骨骼與軟組織肥大。

課本：

**🟢 判定：不完全正確 —— 少了 lymphatic（L）這一成分。課本的定義用字不是「三徵」，而是「CLVM ＋ overgrowth」。**

**1. 課本原始定義用字**

- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕原文：
  > "Klippel–Trénaunay syndrome (KTS) denotes a **slow-flow, capillary–lymphatic–venous malformation (CLVM) of an extremity in association with soft-tissue and/or skeletal overgrowth**; the condition is caused by a PIK3CA mutation."
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕原文（列於 Lymphatic Malformation → PROS 段落內）：
  > "Klippel-Trénaunay syndrome is a **capillary-lymphatic-venous malformation of an extremity with overgrowth**."

→ **兩本一致**：課本的組成是 **C（capillary）＋ L（lymphatic）＋ V（venous）三種畸形，再加上 overgrowth**。使用者原本的「三徵」把 **lymphatic 漏掉**，改成把 marginal vein 塞進第②項。口試若被問「KTS 三個成分是什麼」，正確答案是 **capillary + lymphatic + venous**（不是 capillary + venous + overgrowth）。

**2. 各成分的課本描述**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

- **Capillary 成分**：
  > "The capillary malformation is distributed in a **geographic pattern over the lateral side of the extremity, buttock, or thorax**. Whereas the capillary malformation is typically macular in a neonate, later it becomes **studded with hemolymphatic vesicles**."
- **Venous 成分**：
  > "The venous component of CLVM manifests as **abnormal drainage of the affected area**."
- **Lymphatic 成分**：
  > "The lymphatic abnormalities are typically **macrocystic in the pelvis and thighs and microcystic in the abdominal wall, buttock, and distal limb**."
- **Overgrowth**：
  > "…in association with soft-tissue and/or skeletal overgrowth. There is tremendous variability in the presentation of this disorder, **from a slightly enlarged extremity with a capillary stain to a grotesquely enlarged limb with malformed digits**."

**3. marginal vein of Servelle —— 課本把它放在「影像所見」而不是「三徵」裡**

- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕原文：
  > "MRI is obtained to confirm the diagnosis and determine the extent of the anomalies. **A large, embryonal vein in the subcutaneous tissue (the marginal vein of Servelle) is often located in the lateral calf and thigh and communicates with the deep venous system.**"
- 注意課本用字是 **"marginal vein of Servelle"**（沒有 "lateral" 這個形容詞在名稱裡），位置是 **lateral calf and thigh**，性質是 **large embryonal subcutaneous vein**，且 **與深層靜脈系統相通（communicates with the deep venous system）**。
- ⚠ 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕**全章未出現 marginal vein / Servelle**（Grabb 對 KTS 只有上述一句話）。

**4. 其他課本有寫、使用者沒列的解剖分布數字**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

- 下肢 **95%**、上肢 **5%**、軀幹最少見。
- 「有時對側的足或手也會肥大，常伴 macrodactylous component，且常常沒有 capillary stain。」
- **10% 的 KTS 患者患肢反而是 hypoplastic（發育不良、比較小）**——這點很容易被考官拿來刁難。
- 骨盆受侵犯在下肢 CLVM 很常見，通常無症狀，但可有 **hematuria、bladder outlet obstruction、cystitis、hematochezia**；上肢或軀幹 CLVM 可侵犯 posterior mediastinum 與 retropleural space，但很少有症狀。

### §7.2 跟 Parkes Weber 怎麼分 🟢
我的認知：**KTS 是 slow-flow、沒有動靜脈分流**；Parkes Weber 有 AV shunt（fast-flow），摸得到 thrill、聽得到 bruit。基因 KTS = PIK3CA、PWS = RASA1／EPHB4。

課本：

**🟢 逐項判定**

| 使用者敘述 | 判定 | 課本依據 |
|---|---|---|
| KTS 是 slow-flow | **✅ 對** | 〔Neligan〕"KTS denotes a **slow-flow**, capillary–lymphatic–venous malformation" |
| KTS 沒有 AV shunt | **✅ 對（但課本是用 slow-flow 反面表述，未直接寫 "no AV shunt"）** | 見下註 |
| PWS 有 AV shunt、fast-flow | **✅ 對** | 〔Neligan〕"PWS is a **diffuse AVM in an overgrown extremity with an overlying CM**"；"patients have **microshunting in muscle**"；"Angiography demonstrates discrete arteriovenous shunts." |
| 摸得到 thrill、聽得到 bruit | **✅ 對，且是課本的確診依據** | 〔Neligan〕"**The diagnosis is confirmed by the detection of a bruit or thrill.**" |
| KTS ＝ PIK3CA | **✅ 對** | 〔Neligan〕"the condition is caused by a **PIK3CA mutation**"；〔Grabb〕KTS 列在 PROS 之下 |
| PWS ＝ RASA1／EPHB4 | **✅ 對** | 〔Neligan〕"the disorder is caused by mutations in **RASA1 or EPHB4**"；〔Grabb〕"**autosomal dominant germline mutations in RASA1 or EPHB4**" |

**1. 「課本有沒有把 KTS 歸進 PROS？」→ 有，兩本都有，而且是明文標題層級。**

- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕**獨立小標題就叫「PIK3CA-related overgrowth spectrum (PROS)」**，其下第一個子標題就是 **Klippel–Trénaunay syndrome**，接著是 CLOVES syndrome、Macrocephaly–capillary malformation。PROS 段落原文：
  > "The acronym PROS indicates a wide range of phenotypes caused by mutations in PIK3CA and several have associated vascular malformations."
  - 該章的基因總表中，**PROS 那一列直接列出：CLOVES／Klippel–Trénaunay／Facial infiltrating lipomatosis／Megalencephaly–capillary malformation，基因 PIK3CA，體細胞（Somatic）突變。**
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕原文（在 Lymphatic Malformation 段）：
  > "**PIK3CA-related overgrowth spectrum (PROS) represents a wide range of phenotypes and several have associated LMs.** Klippel-Trénaunay syndrome is a capillary-lymphatic-venous malformation of an extremity with overgrowth. CLOVES syndrome is defined by Congenital Lipomatosis Overgrowth, Vascular malformations, Epidermal nevi, and Scoliosis. Facial infiltrating lipomatosis causes overgrowth of one side of the face often with mucosal neuromas."

**2. PWS 的課本完整描述**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕（可直接背）

> "Parkes Weber syndrome (PWS) is a diffuse AVM in an overgrown extremity with an overlying CM; the disorder is caused by mutations in RASA1 or EPHB4. **PWS involves the lower extremity approximately twice as often as the upper extremity**; patients have **microshunting in muscle**. The malformation is **evident at birth with symmetric enlargement and pink staining of the involved limb**. The cutaneous stain tends to be **confluent rather than patchy and is typically warmer than a banal capillary malformation**. **The diagnosis is confirmed by the detection of a bruit or thrill.** MRI is obtained to evaluate the extent of the malformation. Overgrowth in an affected extremity is **subcutaneous, muscular, and bony with diffuse microfistulas**. The enlarged limb muscles and bones exhibit an abnormal signal and enhancement. Angiography demonstrates discrete arteriovenous shunts."

**3. PWS 與 CM-AVM 的從屬關係（課本有寫，這是進階加分點）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

> "Capillary malformation–arteriovenous malformation (CM-AVM) is an **autosomal dominant** condition caused by a **loss-of-function mutation in the RASA1 or EPHB4 genes**; the prevalence is **1 in 100,000 Caucasians**. … A total of **30% also have an AVM: Parkes Weber syndrome (PWS) (12%), extracerebral AVM (11%), or intracerebral AVM (7%)**. … Because **7% of patients with CM-AVM will have an intracranial fast-flow lesion, brain MRI should be considered.**"
- CM-AVM 的 CM 型態：**small, multifocal, round, pinkish-red, and often surrounded by a pale halo**（50%）。

**4. ⚠ 兩本／同一本內部的不一致（口試若被追問要能講）**

- **PWS 的遺傳模式標註不一致**：
  - 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕內文：PWS 是 "**autosomal dominant germline mutations** in RASA1 or EPHB4"。
  - 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕內文只寫 "caused by mutations in RASA1 or EPHB4"，但**該章基因總表把 Parkes Weber syndrome 標為「Somatic」**，而同表中 CM-AVM 標為「Dominant」。
  - **裁決建議**：以「**germline RASA1／EPHB4，autosomal dominant，屬 CM-AVM 光譜的一個表現型**」回答（此為 Grabb 內文＋Neligan CM-AVM 內文一致的說法）；Neligan 表格的 "Somatic" 應視為表格標註不一致，不要當作主答案。

**5. KTS vs PWS 一句話對比（可直接背的課本用字）**

- **KTS**＝ *slow-flow*, **C–L–V** malformation of an extremity ＋ overgrowth，**PIK3CA**（PROS）。
- **PWS**＝ *diffuse AVM*（fast-flow, microshunting in muscle）in an overgrown extremity ＋ overlying CM，**RASA1／EPHB4**（CM-AVM 光譜）。
- ⚠ 課本**沒有**寫出「KTS 完全沒有 AV shunt」這句話；它是用 **slow-flow** 一詞界定，並以 PWS 的 "bruit or thrill" 作為分辨點。回答時建議用課本語言：「KTS 是 slow-flow，臨床上沒有 thrill／bruit；PWS 是 fast-flow，靠 bruit or thrill 確診。」

### §7.3 檢查 🟡
我的認知：MRI／MRV、duplex ultrasound；**最關鍵的是確認深層靜脈系統是否通暢**。

課本：

**🟡 課本列的檢查（逐項照抄）**

1. **MRI —— 這是課本唯一明講的 KTS 影像**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕
   > "**MRI is obtained to confirm the diagnosis and determine the extent of the anomalies.** A large, embryonal vein in the subcutaneous tissue (the marginal vein of Servelle) is often located in the lateral calf and thigh and communicates with the deep venous system."
2. **Plain radiography（下肢長度追蹤）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕
   > "**By 2 years of age, radiologic surveillance of leg length by plain radiography is indicated.**"
3. **不需要做的檢查（課本明文否定）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕
   > "**Unlike some other hemihypertrophy syndromes, patients with KTS are not at increased risk for Wilms tumor and screening ultrasonography is unnecessary.**"
   → 這句很可能是考點：**KTS 不必做 Wilms tumor 的腹部超音波篩檢**（與 Beckwith–Wiedemann 等 hemihypertrophy 症候群不同）。
4. **一般 vascular anomalies 的影像原則**〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕
   > "Most vascular anomalies can be diagnosed by history and physical examination. If the type of lesion remains unclear, then imaging is indicated, **typically ultrasound followed by MRI**. Less than 1% of vascular anomalies require histopathology or genetic testing for diagnosis."
5. **VM 成分的影像特徵（可借用來描述 KTS 的 V 成分）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕
   > "VM is **hyperintense on T2-weighted sequences**. In contrast to LM, **VM enhances with contrast, often shows phleboliths as signal voids, and is more likely to involve muscle**. US findings include **compressible, anechoic–hypoechoic channels** separated by more solid regions of variable echogenicity. **Phleboliths are hyperechoic with acoustic shadowing.** CT is occasionally indicated to assess osseous VM."

**⚠ 使用者認知中查無出處的三點**

- **MRV**：⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 Vascular anomalies、Grabb 9e Ch25 Vascular Anomalies、Grabb 9e Ch100 Vascular Disorders of the Upper Extremity；關鍵字 MRV／magnetic resonance venography／venography 全章 0 命中）。Neligan Ch31 只寫 **MRI**。
- **duplex ultrasound 用於 KTS**：⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Neligan Ch31 全章 "duplex" 僅 1 次，且出現在 PHACE／LUMBAR 段落的 "solitary/duplex kidney"，與 duplex 超音波無關；Grabb Ch25 只寫 "ultrasound followed by MRI" 的泛論。唯一有 "duplex ultrasound" 字樣者為 Grabb 9e Ch100，但那是 hypothenar hammer syndrome 的評估，與 KTS 無關）。
- **「最關鍵的是確認深層靜脈系統是否通暢」**：**⚠ 課本查無此明文，且 Neligan 的敘述方向相反** —— 見 §7.5 紅線段。Neligan 原文是：
  > "**A functioning deep venous system is present**, although it is often difficult to visualize because of predominant flow in the superficial veins."
  課本把「深層靜脈系統是通的」當作 **KTS 的既定事實（premise）**，而不是「開刀前必須先證明的前提條件（prerequisite）」。

### §7.4 併發症 🟡
我的認知：localized intravascular coagulopathy（LIC，D-dimer 上升）、PE 風險、肢體長度差、出血、反覆蜂窩組織炎。

課本：

**🟡 課本明列的 KTS 併發症（含數字，直接照抄）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

> "Complications include **thrombophlebitis (20%–45%) and pulmonary embolism (4%–24%)**."

- **Thrombophlebitis：20–45%**
- **Pulmonary embolism：4–24%**
- **Leg-length discrepancy（肢體長度差）**：課本以治療門檻方式呈現 —— "By 2 years of age, radiologic surveillance of leg length by plain radiography is indicated. **If the discrepancy is >1.5 cm, a shoe-lift for the shorter limb can prevent limping and scoliosis.**"
- **皮膚 vesicle 出血／滲液**："Excision and grafting is occasionally necessary for **diffuse bleeding and/or oozing cutaneous vesicles**."（皮膚上的 hemolymphatic vesicles 是出血來源）
- **骨盆／內臟受侵犯的症狀**："It is usually asymptomatic, although **hematuria, bladder outlet obstruction, cystitis, and hematochezia** can occur."
- **足部過度肥大**："Enlargement of the foot may require a **ray, midfoot, or Syme amputation** to allow the use of footwear."
- **明文否定的併發症**："patients with KTS are **not at increased risk for Wilms tumor**."

**LIC 在課本裡的位置：寫在 Venous malformation 一般段落，不是寫在 KTS 段落**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

> "A large VM involving the **deep venous system is at risk for thrombosis and pulmonary embolism**. Gastrointestinal VM can cause bleeding and chronic anemia. **Stagnation within a large VM results in a localized intravascular coagulopathy (LIC) and painful phlebothromboses.**"

> "Large lesions are at risk for **coagulation of stagnant blood, stimulation of thrombin, and conversion of fibrinogen to fibrin**. **LIC can become disseminated intravascular coagulopathy (DIC) following trauma or therapeutic intervention.** The chronic consumptive coagulopathy can cause either **thrombosis (phleboliths) or bleeding (hemarthrosis, hematoma, intraoperative blood loss)**."

〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕同義敘述：
> "A VM involving the deep venous system is at risk for thrombosis and pulmonary embolism. **Stagnation within a VM results in a localized intravascular coagulopathy and painful phlebothromboses.**"
> "**Low-molecular-weight heparin is considered for patients with extensive VMs with localized intravascular coagulopathy who can develop disseminated intravascular coagulopathy.** Patients at risk for a serious thrombotic event require long-term anticoagulation or a vena cava filter."

**其他 VM 相關併發症（課本列表，可補充）**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕
> "Complications of VM include **pain, swelling, and psychosocial issues**. Head and neck VM may present with **mucosal bleeding or progressive distortion leading to airway or orbital compromise**. Extremity VM can cause **leg-length discrepancy, hypoplasia due to disuse atrophy, pathologic fracture, hemarthrosis, and degenerative arthritis**. VM of muscle may result in **fibrosis and subsequent pain and disability**."

**⚠ 使用者認知中查無出處的兩點**

- **D-dimer 上升**：⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31、Grabb 9e Ch25，關鍵字 "D-dimer" 全章 **0 命中**）。課本描述 LIC 的機轉是「fibrinogen→fibrin、消耗性凝血病、可轉為 DIC」，但**沒有提到任何實驗室指標（D-dimer／fibrinogen 數值）**。口試回答 D-dimer 不算錯（臨床標準做法），但要知道**這不是這兩本課本的文字**。
- **反覆蜂窩組織炎（recurrent cellulitis）**：⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：Neligan Ch31 KTS 段與 LM 段、Grabb Ch25；KTS 併發症清單只列 thrombophlebitis 與 PE。Grabb Ch25 在 **Lymphatic Malformation** 泛論段有 "Complications include deformity, bleeding, ulceration, and **infection**"，但那是 LM 的通則，並未指名 KTS 的反覆蜂窩組織炎）。

### §7.5 ⭐ 治療與那條紅線 🔴
我的認知：
- 保守為主：壓力衣、抬高
- 硬化治療；port-wine stain 用染料雷射
- **切除 lateral marginal vein 的前提是深層靜脈系統通暢** —— 深層若阻塞而切掉表淺靜脈會是災難
- 肢體長度差 >2 cm → epiphysiodesis
- 近年：sirolimus；PIK3CA inhibitor（alpelisib）
要確認：課本的治療段落怎麼寫？sirolimus／alpelisib 有沒有進課本？

課本：

---

#### 🔴【必背】Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 —— KTS 治療原文整段（一字不漏）

> "By 2 years of age, radiologic surveillance of leg length by plain radiography is indicated. **If the discrepancy is >1.5 cm, a shoe-lift for the shorter limb can prevent limping and scoliosis. Epiphysiodesis of the distal femoral growth plate is typically done around 11 years of age.** Enlargement of the foot may require a ray, midfoot, or Syme amputation to allow the use of footwear. **Management of the VM component is conservative with compressive stockings for insufficiency and aspirin to minimize phlebothrombosis. Symptomatic varicose veins may be removed or sclerosed. Sclerotherapy may be necessary for focal macrocystic lymphatic malformation or to treat cutaneous vesicles. Excision and grafting is occasionally necessary for diffuse bleeding and/or oozing cutaneous vesicles. Circumferential overgrowth may be managed by staged contour resection. Venous insufficiency does not occur following staged subcutaneous excision or removal of the marginal vein of Servelle. A functioning deep venous system is present, although it is often difficult to visualize because of predominant flow in the superficial veins.**"

---

#### 🔴【核心裁決】那條「紅線」—— 課本**沒有**明文寫

**問題：「切除 lateral marginal vein 的前提是深層靜脈系統通暢」課本有沒有明文寫？**

**答：⚠ 沒有。兩本課本都查無此明文（已查：Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 Vascular anomalies 全章、Grabb 9e Ch25 Vascular Anomalies 全章、Grabb 9e Ch100；關鍵字 marginal vein／Servelle／deep venous／patency 逐一比對）。而且 Neligan 的原文語氣與這條紅線的「前提條件」框架不同，甚至方向相反。**

**課本原文只有這兩句（緊接在一起）**：

> "**Venous insufficiency does not occur following staged subcutaneous excision or removal of the marginal vein of Servelle. A functioning deep venous system is present, although it is often difficult to visualize because of predominant flow in the superficial veins.**"

**這兩句課本語意的精確拆解（口試若被追問，要照這個講）：**

1. 課本是在**做出保證（reassurance）**，不是在**設下條件（prerequisite）**：它說「切除 marginal vein of Servelle 之後**不會**發生 venous insufficiency」。
2. 課本給的理由是：**KTS 病人的深層靜脈系統本來就是通的（"A functioning deep venous system is present"）** —— 課本把這件事寫成 KTS 的**既定事實／通則**。
3. 課本進一步解釋為什麼臨床上會誤以為深層靜脈不通：**"it is often difficult to visualize because of predominant flow in the superficial veins"** —— 因為血流大量走表淺靜脈，深部系統在影像上**顯影不良**，容易被誤判為「深層阻塞」。
4. 課本也明文寫了 marginal vein 與深部系統的解剖關係：**"the marginal vein of Servelle … communicates with the deep venous system"**。

**⚠ 衝突與裁決（強烈建議寫進手冊的「衝突與裁決」欄）**

- **使用者的紅線（＝台灣考場與傳統血管外科教學的常見說法）**：切除表淺／marginal vein **之前必須先確認深層靜脈通暢**，否則會造成災難性的靜脈回流障礙。
- **Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 的立場**：**deep venous system 本來就是 functioning 的**，切除 marginal vein of Servelle **不會**造成 venous insufficiency，影像上看不到深部系統是**顯影問題而非阻塞**。
- **兩者不是互相矛盾的臨床行為，而是「舉證責任」放在哪一邊的差別**：Neligan 把「深部通暢」當**預設**；傳統教學把它當**須先證明的前提**。
- **口試建議答法（兩邊都涵蓋，最安全）**：
  > 「切除 marginal vein of Servelle 之前我會先以 MRI 評估深層靜脈系統。依 Neligan 第五版的說法，KTS 病人的深層靜脈系統其實是 functioning 的，只是因為血流優先走表淺靜脈而不易顯影，因此**分期的皮下切除或切除 marginal vein of Servelle 並不會造成靜脈功能不全**；但臨床上仍應先確認深部系統的存在與通暢，避免把唯一的回流路徑切掉。」
  —— 這樣既抄到課本原文（考官若拿 Neligan 對答案，你贏），又保留臨床安全論述（考官若是傳統派，你也沒錯）。

---

#### 🔴 治療各項逐條對照

**① 保守治療：壓力衣 ✅／抬高 ⚠**

- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕KTS 段原文：
  > "**Management of the VM component is conservative with compressive stockings for insufficiency and aspirin to minimize phlebothrombosis.**"
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕VM 通則段（可補充，含 aspirin 劑量）：
  > "Patients with an extensive extremity VM are prescribed **custom-fitted compression garments** to reduce **blood stagnation and minimize expansion, LIC, phlebolith formation, and pain**. Patients with recurrent pain secondary to phlebothrombosis are given **prophylactic daily aspirin (81 mg)** to prevent thrombosis."
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕：
  > "Patients with recurrent pain secondary to phlebothrombosis are given **aspirin** to prevent thrombosis."
- **⚠「抬高（elevation）」**：課本查無明確出處（已查：Neligan Ch31 KTS 段與 VM 段、Grabb Ch25 —— KTS／VM 的保守治療只寫 **compression stockings/garments ＋ aspirin**，沒有 elevation）。唯一提到 elevation 者是〔Grabb 9e Ch100〕論上肢 **lymphatic malformation**：「They do shrink with elevation」，並非 KTS 的治療建議。**壓力衣是課本明文，抬高不是。**

**② 硬化治療（sclerotherapy）✅ —— 但要注意課本指定的適應症**

- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕KTS 段：
  > "**Symptomatic varicose veins may be removed or sclerosed. Sclerotherapy may be necessary for focal macrocystic lymphatic malformation or to treat cutaneous vesicles.**"
  → 課本在 KTS 的 sclerotherapy 有**兩個明確標的**：**症狀性靜脈曲張**、以及 **focal macrocystic LM／皮膚 vesicles**。
- VM 通則（若被追問 sclerosant 與療效數字）〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕：
  > "First-line treatment is **sclerotherapy, which is safer and more effective than resection**. **Good to excellent results are obtained in 75%–90% of patients.** … Sclerotherapy is repeated until symptoms are alleviated or when injectable vascular spaces are no longer present."
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕：
  > "Sclerotherapy is **repeated every 6 weeks** until injectable vascular spaces are no longer present. The most commonly used sclerosant is **sodium tetradecyl sulfate**." … "**Almost all VMs should have sclerotherapy prior to operative intervention.** After sclerotherapy, the VM is replaced by scar and thus the risk of **blood loss, iatrogenic injury, and recurrence** is reduced."
- LM 常用 sclerosant〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕：**doxycycline, sodium tetradecyl sulfate, ethanol, bleomycin**；〔Neligan〕補充："**Ethanol is an effective sclerosant but has the highest complication rate.**"

**③ port-wine stain 用染料雷射 ✅**

- 〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕：
  > "**Treatment of CM is pulsed dye laser to lighten its color and slow progression.** Intervention in infancy achieves superior results and reduces the risk of subsequent darkening and hypertrophy… **Multiple treatments, spaced 6 weeks apart, are often required until the CM fails to improve.**"
- 〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕：
  > "**Pulsed-dye laser therapy can improve the appearance of CM by lightening the color; the head and neck region responds better than the extremities.** Outcome also is superior for smaller lesions and those treated at a younger age. A **higher rate of complications, including pigmentary changes and hypertrophic scarring, is reported in individuals with darker skin. After pulsed-dye laser treatment, CM often re-darkens over time.**"
  → **注意：課本明講四肢的 CM 對 PDL 反應比頭頸差**，而 KTS 的 CM 正是在四肢 —— 這是很好的加分點。
- 〔Grabb 9e Ch21 Lasers〕（雷射參數）：
  > "The most used laser to target vascular lesions is the **pulsed dye laser (PDL). PDLs range in wavelength from 585 to 600 nm.** These are highly absorbed by **oxyhemoglobin** and are the **standard of care for hemangiomas and capillary malformations**." … "**Without treatment, 65% of PWSs become hypertrophic**, often leading to bleeding, esthetic deformation, and psychological stress." … 章末解答："**The 595-nm PDL has represented the standard of treatment for PWSs over the past 30 years.**"
  → 其他選項：**KTP 532 nm** 與 **IPL** 也有效。成人 PWS 因 deoxyhemoglobin 較多、顏色偏深紫，需要**較長波長**的雷射。

**④ ⚠ 肢體長度差的數字 —— 使用者的「>2 cm → epiphysiodesis」與課本不符**

**課本原文（Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31）：**
> "By 2 years of age, radiologic surveillance of leg length by plain radiography is indicated. **If the discrepancy is >1.5 cm, a shoe-lift for the shorter limb can prevent limping and scoliosis. Epiphysiodesis of the distal femoral growth plate is typically done around 11 years of age.**"

**逐項更正：**

| 項目 | 使用者認知 | 課本 |
|---|---|---|
| 追蹤起點 | 未提 | **2 歲起以 plain radiography 監測 leg length** |
| 數字門檻 **1.5 cm** 的用途 | 誤記為 2 cm → epiphysiodesis | **>1.5 cm → 給短側墊高鞋（shoe-lift）**，目的是預防 **limping 與 scoliosis** |
| epiphysiodesis 的時機 | 誤記為「長度差 >2 cm」 | **課本用「年齡」而非「公分數」界定：約 11 歲，做 distal femoral growth plate 的 epiphysiodesis** |
| 部位 | 未提 | **distal femoral growth plate（遠端股骨生長板）** |

- ⚠ **「>2 cm 做 epiphysiodesis」課本查無出處**（已查：Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 全章、Grabb 9e Ch25 全章；"epiphysiodesis" 在 Grabb Ch25 **0 命中**，在 Neligan Ch31 僅上引 1 處且**未給任何公分門檻**）。
- **口試安全講法**：「2 歲起用 X 光追蹤下肢長度；**差距超過 1.5 公分先用墊高鞋**預防跛行與脊椎側彎；若持續惡化，**約 11 歲時做遠端股骨生長板的 epiphysiodesis**。」

**⑤ 手術：分期輪廓切除與截肢**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕

> "**Circumferential overgrowth may be managed by staged contour resection.**"
> "**Enlargement of the foot may require a ray, midfoot, or Syme amputation to allow the use of footwear.**"
> "Excision and grafting is occasionally necessary for **diffuse bleeding and/or oozing cutaneous vesicles**."

- 一般 VM 手術原則（Grabb 9e Ch25，切除策略的關鍵句）：
  > "**Subtotal removal of a large, diffuse VM is preferred, rather than attempting "complete" excision of a benign lesion that might result in a worse deformity than the VM.**"
- Grabb 總則（很適合當作 KTS 治療哲學的開場白）：
  > "**Treatment of a vascular anomaly is not mandatory, and patients often can be observed. Intervention is based on symptoms and should not cause worse morbidity than the lesion.** For example, **targeted treatment of symptomatic areas typically is preferred to radical resections and complex reconstructions for a benign condition.**"

**⑥ ⭐ sirolimus 與 alpelisib —— 有進課本，但兩本的寫法不同，必須分清楚**

**〔Grabb 9e Ch25〕—— 有明文，且明確涵蓋 PROS（KTS 屬 PROS）**

> "LM was the first vascular malformation to be successfully treated with drug therapy using **sirolimus, which is a mTOR inhibitor that blocks PIK3CA signaling**. Because sirolimus typically is well-tolerated, our center increasingly administers the drug along with other treatments (eg, sclerotherapy, resection) to maximize treatment response and reduce recurrence. **PROS conditions and LM also are being treated successfully in our center and others with sirolimus and the PIK3CA inhibitor alpelisib.**"

> （VM 段）"Recently, patients with severe VMs are being treated with **oral sirolimus and alpelisib**, which have shown efficacy."

> （章首 Table 25.1 主要 vascular anomalies 對照表）**Venous malformation → 治療欄明列：Sclerotherapy／Excision／Sirolimus／Alpelisib**；Lymphatic → Sclerotherapy、Radiofrequency ablation、CO2 laser、Bleomycin injection、Excision；Arteriovenous → Embolization、Excision、**Trametinib**。

> （結論段）"**Most vascular malformations are now able to be treated with pharmacotherapy.**" … "In the past, pharmacotherapy for vascular malformations did not exist. **We now have drugs for LM, VM, and AVMs.** Management of patients is transitioning toward a similar paradigm to treating malignancies."

**〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31〕—— sirolimus 有內文，alpelisib 只出現在參考文獻，未進內文**

> （LM Pharmacotherapy 段）"**Oral sirolimus has shown efficacy for LMs.** Generally, this drug is used for **diffuse lesions that are not amenable or have failed other therapies**. Because the medication typically is well-tolerated, the indications for its use have expanded. **Our center often administers the drug along with other treatments (e.g., sclerotherapy, resection) in an effort to maximize response and reduce recurrence.**"

> （結論段）"The future of the field of vascular anomalies is exciting because the mutation responsible for most lesions has been identified over the last several years. Consequently, our understanding of the etiopathogenesis of these lesions has improved and **led to novel pharmacotherapy for patients with vascular malformations.**"（此處引用的參考文獻 115–121 即包含 **低劑量 sirolimus 治療 PROS**〔Parker VER et al., Genet Med 2019〕、**PIK3CA-related overgrowth syndrome 的 targeted therapy**〔Venot Q et al., Nature 2018〕、以及 **alpelisib 治療 CLOVES**〔López Gutiérrez JC et al., 2019〕）

> （Algorithm 31.2 血管畸形治療流程圖）**Lymphatic malformation → Sclerotherapy／Bleomycin injection／Carbon dioxide laser／Radiofrequency ablation／Resection／Sirolimus**；Venous → Sclerotherapy／Resection；Capillary → Pulsed-dye laser／Resection；AVM → Embolization／Resection。
> ⚠ 注意：**Neligan 的 Algorithm 31.2 中，Venous malformation 欄位並未列入 sirolimus**（只有 LM 有）。

**裁決：**
- **sirolimus**：**兩本皆有明文**。Neligan 主要掛在 **LM**；Grabb 更進一步，把 sirolimus 用在 **LM、VM、以及 PROS（KTS 所屬光譜）**。
- **alpelisib（PIK3CA inhibitor）**：**只有 Grabb 9e Ch25 有內文明文**（用於 severe VM，以及 **PROS conditions**）；**Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch31 內文完全沒有出現 alpelisib 這個字**，僅見於文末參考文獻 121（CLOVES 個案）。
- **⚠ 兩本都沒有在「Klippel–Trénaunay」那一段直接寫 sirolimus 或 alpelisib**。KTS 用藥的根據是**間接推論**：KTS 屬 PROS → Grabb 明寫 "PROS conditions … are being treated successfully … with sirolimus and the PIK3CA inhibitor alpelisib"。
- **口試建議答法**：「KTS 屬於 PIK3CA-related overgrowth spectrum。Grabb 第九版明文指出 **PROS 的病人已可用 sirolimus（mTOR inhibitor，阻斷 PIK3CA 訊息傳遞）與 PIK3CA 抑制劑 alpelisib 成功治療**；Neligan 第五版則將 sirolimus 主要寫在 lymphatic malformation 的藥物治療，alpelisib 僅見於其參考文獻。」—— 這樣講可以同時展示「知道課本寫到哪裡、也知道課本沒寫到哪裡」。

## 查證完成說明

這份是回填版。查證方式：只讀 Google Drive 中已拆分的 **Grabb & Smith 9e 章節 PDF** 與 **Neligan 5e 各 Vol 章節 PDF**，未使用整本書 PDF、未使用學會題庫 docx、未使用網路，也未用模型自身記憶補洞。

- 每條答案後面都標了出處（例：`〔Grabb 9e Ch29〕`、`〔Neligan 5e Vol3 Ch21.11〕`）。
- 兩本書不一致的地方一律並列，並註明哪本說什麼。
- 課本真的沒有寫的，標成 `⚠ 課本查無明確出處（已查：…）`，後面列出實際查過的章與搜過的關鍵字。這種項目**請照手冊慣例標「課本找不到明確出處，被追問時不要硬掰」**，不要當成課本內容寫進手冊。

### 需要特別注意的「課本整段沒有」的項目
1. **§3.2 Stanford protocol（Phase I／II）** — 兩本書全部章節零命中。
2. **§5.1 UHMS 核可適應症清單** 與 **§5.2 治療參數／Marx protocol 30+10** — 兩本書全部章節零命中，連 `ATA`、`UHMS` 字串都沒有。
3. **§4.4 Adams 14-point plan 的 14 個條目** — 查無；課本只有 Neligan Vol5 Box 7.1 的 8 條，已改抄該版本。
4. **§2.7 骨骼成熟度的影像判定法（hand-wrist film／CVM）** — 查無，課本只給年齡。
5. **§2.3 Wits appraisal／FMA／MP-SN 正常值** — 查無。
6. **§7.5「切除 marginal vein 前必須先確認深層靜脈通暢」** — 查無此前提句；Neligan 的語氣反而是「深部本來就通，切了不會造成 venous insufficiency」。這一條是本次最大的認知落差，請務必用衝突並列的體例處理。
